            assert stored.status == "queued"
            assert stored.attempt_count == 0
            assert adapter.attempted_batches == []
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_sql_envelope_adapter_is_insert_only_idempotent_and_reconciled() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE downstream_envelopes ("
            "business_idempotency_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, "
            "mechanism_version_id INTEGER NOT NULL, "
            "payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL, payload_json TEXT NOT NULL, "
            "external_record_key TEXT NOT NULL, "
            "revision INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1, "
            "review_state TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'auto', "
            "published_at DATETIME NULL)"
        )
    adapter = SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter(
        connection_factory=engine.begin,
        table_name="downstream_envelopes",
        evidence=DownstreamSafetyEvidence(
            least_privileged=True,
            schema_fingerprint="f" * 64,
            verifier="fixture",
        ),
    )
    key = DeliveryKey("knowledge_graph:sql-a", 7, "a" * 64)
    batch = DeliveryBatch(
        target_contract_key="knowledge-graph-output",
        contract_version=1,
        items=(
            DeliveryItem(
                event_id=1,
                key=key,
                payload={"content_id": "sql-a", "result": {"value": "ok"}},
            ),
        ),
    )
    try:
        first = adapter.deliver(batch)
        second = adapter.deliver(batch)
        readback = adapter.read_back((key,))

        assert first.results[0].succeeded is True
        assert second.results[0].succeeded is True
        assert readback[0].key == key
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT count(*) FROM downstream_envelopes"
            ).scalar_one() == 1

        drifted = DeliveryBatch(
            target_contract_key=batch.target_contract_key,
            contract_version=batch.contract_version,
            items=(
                DeliveryItem(
                    event_id=2,
                    key=DeliveryKey(key.business_idempotency_key, 8, "b" * 64),
                    payload={"content_id": "sql-a", "result": {"value": "changed"}},
                ),
            ),
        )
        conflict = adapter.deliver(drifted)
        assert conflict.results[0].succeeded is False
        assert conflict.results[0].error_code == "downstream_identity_conflict"
        assert adapter.read_back((key,))[0].key == key
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_sql_envelope_adapter_overwrites_in_place_with_revision_guard() -> None:
    """ADR-0064 先发后审：同一素材更高 revision 原地覆盖；旧版本迟到、自动覆盖已审均被护栏拒绝。"""

    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE downstream_envelopes ("
            "business_idempotency_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, "
            "mechanism_version_id INTEGER NOT NULL, "
            "payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL, payload_json TEXT NOT NULL, "
            "external_record_key TEXT NOT NULL, "
            "revision INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1, "
            "review_state TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'auto', "
            "published_at DATETIME NULL)"
        )
    adapter = SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter(
        connection_factory=engine.begin,
        table_name="downstream_envelopes",
        evidence=DownstreamSafetyEvidence(
            least_privileged=True,
            schema_fingerprint="f" * 64,
            verifier="fixture",
        ),
    )

    def batch(key: DeliveryKey, value: str) -> DeliveryBatch:
        return DeliveryBatch(
            target_contract_key="knowledge-graph-output",
            contract_version=1,
            items=(
                DeliveryItem(
                    event_id=1,
                    key=key,
                    payload={"content_id": "sql-a", "result": {"value": value}},
                ),
            ),
        )

    identity = "knowledge_graph:sql-a"
    auto_v1 = DeliveryKey(identity, 7, "a" * 64, revision=1, review_state="auto")
    auto_v2 = DeliveryKey(identity, 8, "b" * 64, revision=2, review_state="auto")
    reviewed_v3 = DeliveryKey(
        identity, 8, "c" * 64, revision=3, review_state="reviewed"
    )
    auto_v4 = DeliveryKey(identity, 9, "d" * 64, revision=4, review_state="auto")
    try:
        assert adapter.deliver(batch(auto_v1, "first")).results[0].succeeded
        # 新机制版本重跑：更高 revision 原地覆盖，仍只有一行
        assert adapter.deliver(batch(auto_v2, "second")).results[0].succeeded
        assert adapter.read_back((auto_v2,))[0].key == auto_v2
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT count(*) FROM downstream_envelopes"
            ).scalar_one() == 1
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT revision, review_state FROM downstream_envelopes"
            ).one() == (2, "auto")
        # 旧版本迟到：护栏拒绝，行不变
        stale = adapter.deliver(batch(auto_v1, "first")).results[0]
        assert stale.succeeded is False
        assert stale.error_code == "downstream_stale_revision"
        assert adapter.read_back((auto_v2,))[0].key == auto_v2
        # 人工纠偏重发：覆盖为 reviewed
        assert adapter.deliver(batch(reviewed_v3, "human")).results[0].succeeded
        assert adapter.read_back((reviewed_v3,))[0].key == reviewed_v3
        # 自动结果不能覆盖人工已审行
        guarded = adapter.deliver(batch(auto_v4, "auto-again")).results[0]
        assert guarded.succeeded is False
        assert guarded.error_code == "downstream_review_guard"
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT revision, review_state, payload_json FROM downstream_envelopes"
            ).one()[:2] == (3, "reviewed")
        # 同 revision 内容不同仍是身份冲突（fail-closed）
        conflict = adapter.deliver(
            batch(DeliveryKey(identity, 8, "e" * 64, revision=3, review_state="reviewed"), "x")
        ).results[0]
        assert conflict.error_code == "downstream_identity_conflict"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mysql_sql_envelope_adapter_uses_insert_ignore_and_readback(
    mysql_engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE downstream_envelopes ("
            "business_idempotency_key VARCHAR(240) PRIMARY KEY, "
            "mechanism_version_id BIGINT NOT NULL, "
            "payload_hash CHAR(64) NOT NULL, payload_json LONGTEXT NOT NULL, "
            "external_record_key VARCHAR(240) NOT NULL, "
            "revision BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1, "
            "review_state VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'auto', "
            "published_at DATETIME(6) NULL)"
        )
    adapter = SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter(
        connection_factory=mysql_engine.begin,
        table_name="downstream_envelopes",
        evidence=DownstreamSafetyEvidence(
            least_privileged=True,
            schema_fingerprint="f" * 64,
            verifier="mysql-test",
        ),
    )
    key = DeliveryKey("knowledge_graph:mysql-a", 11, "c" * 64)
    batch = DeliveryBatch(
        target_contract_key="knowledge-graph-output",
        contract_version=1,
        items=(
            DeliveryItem(
                event_id=1,
                key=key,
                payload={"content_id": "mysql-a"},
            ),
        ),
    )

    assert adapter.deliver(batch).results[0].succeeded is True
    assert adapter.deliver(batch).results[0].succeeded is True
    assert adapter.read_back((key,))[0].key == key
    with mysql_engine.connect() as connection:
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT count(*) FROM downstream_envelopes"
        ).scalar_one() == 1


def test_retryable_failure_waits_for_backoff_instead_of_retrying_immediately() -> None:
    # 复审 09-26：_failure 给可重试失败 retry_after_seconds=0，fail_outbox_event
    # 的退避永不生效，下游抖动几秒内就把 5 次打满进死信。
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)
            target = _target(db)
            event = _enqueue(db, version, target, "jitter")
            db.commit()
            adapter = FixtureDownstreamAdapter(
                schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint,
                fail_once_keys={event.business_idempotency_key},
            )
            now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            result = deliver_outbox_batch(
                db, target, worker_id="delivery-worker", adapter=adapter, now=now,
            )
            db.commit()
            stored = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, event.id)
            assert result.retry_event_ids == (event.id,)
            assert stored.status == "queued"
            available = stored.available_at
            if available.tzinfo is None:
                available = available.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            assert available > now
            again = deliver_outbox_batch(
                db, target, worker_id="delivery-worker", adapter=adapter, now=now,
            )
            assert again.delivered_event_ids == () and again.retry_event_ids == ()
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_delivery_lease_outlives_a_whole_batch_of_slow_requests() -> None:
    # 复审 09-26：租约=单次请求超时，而一批最多 batch_size 条、每条都可能耗满超时，
    # 批次结束时租约已过期，complete 抛 outbox_lease_stale 让整个事务回滚——
    # 已被下游接收的事件会被重复提交、尝试次数回滚。租约须覆盖整批最坏耗时。
    from datetime import timedelta

    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism(db)
            target = _target(db, timeout_seconds=30)
            events = [_enqueue(db, version, target, s) for s in ("s1", "s2", "s3")]
            db.commit()
            start = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            clock = {"now": start}

            class SlowAdapter(FixtureDownstreamAdapter):
                def deliver(self, batch):
                    # Three items, each taking the full request timeout.
                    clock["now"] = start + timedelta(seconds=30 * len(batch.items))
                    return super().deliver(batch)

            adapter = SlowAdapter(schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint)
            import app.runtime_outbox as outbox

            real_now = outbox._now
            outbox._now = lambda now=None: clock["now"] if now is None else now
            try:
                result = deliver_outbox_batch(db, target, worker_id="w", adapter=adapter)
            finally:
                outbox._now = real_now
            db.commit()
            assert set(result.delivered_event_ids) == {e.id for e in events}
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_connector_governance_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json

import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.connector_contracts import create_connector_contract_version
from app.connector_intake import (
    ConnectorIntakeError,
    IntakeRequest,
    ingest_upstream_event,
)
from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.models import (
    ConnectorContractVersion,
    RuntimeAuditEvent,
    UpstreamIntakeEvent,
    User,
)


def _client() -> tuple[TestClient, sessionmaker[Session], dict[str, str]]:
    from app.connector_governance_api import build_connector_governance_router

    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    role = {"value": "viewer"}

    def current_user() -> User:
        name = role["value"]
        return User(
            id=1,
            username=name,
            password_hash="x",
            display_name=name,
            role=name,
            is_admin=name == "admin",
        )

    def db_override():
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(build_connector_governance_router(current_user))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = db_override
    return TestClient(app), sessions, role


def _contract_payload(*, fingerprint: str = "a" * 64) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "contract_key": "knowledge-graph-input",
        "direction": "upstream",
        "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
        "logical_locator": "logical.knowledge_graph.input",
        "adapter_key": "api-json-v1",
        "schema_fingerprint": fingerprint,
        "field_mapping": {"content_id": "content_id"},
        "cursor_contract": {
            "field": "sequence",
            "kind": "integer",
            "ordering": "ascending",
            "out_of_order": "delay",
        },
        "idempotency_contract": {"field": "event_id"},
        "batch_size": 100,
        "timeout_seconds": 30,
        "owner": "platform",
        "status": "active",
    }


def test_contract_listing_is_redacted_and_only_admin_creates_versions() -> None:
    client, sessions, role = _client()
    try:
        forbidden = client.post(
            "/api/connector-governance/contracts",
            json=_contract_payload(),
        )
        assert forbidden.status_code == 403
        assert forbidden.json()["detail"]["code"] == (
            "connector_governance_admin_required"
        )

        role["value"] = "admin"
        created = client.post(
            "/api/connector-governance/contracts",
            json=_contract_payload(),
        )
        assert created.status_code == 201, created.text
        assert created.json()["version"] == 1

        next_payload = _contract_payload(fingerprint="b" * 64)
        next_payload.pop("contract_key")
        second = client.post(
            "/api/connector-governance/contracts/knowledge-graph-input/versions",
            json=next_payload,
        )
        assert second.status_code == 201, second.text
        assert second.json()["version"] == 2

        role["value"] = "viewer"
        listing = client.get(
            "/api/connector-governance/contracts"
            "?category_key=knowledge_graph&direction=upstream"
        )
        assert listing.status_code == 200
        assert [item["version"] for item in listing.json()["items"]] == [2, 1]
        serialized = canonical_json(listing.json()).lower()
        for forbidden_term in (
            "password",
            "access_token",
            "api_key",
            "physical_table",
            "connection_string",
            "database_url",
        ):
            assert forbidden_term not in serialized
        assert listing.json()["items"][0]["logical_locator"] == (
            "logical.knowledge_graph.input"
        )
        with sessions() as db:
            assert db.scalar(select(UpstreamIntakeEvent.id)) is None
    finally:
        client.close()


def test_pause_resume_and_replay_append_facts_without_mutating_original() -> None:
    client, sessions, _role = _client()
    _role["value"] = "admin"
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            contract = create_connector_contract_version(
                db,
                created_by="admin",
                **_contract_payload(),
            )
            payload = {"content_id": "asset-9", "sequence": 9}
            payload_json = canonical_json(payload)
            original = UpstreamIntakeEvent(
                contract_version_id=contract.id,
                source_event_id="evt-9",
                source_cursor="9",
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
                mechanism_key="",
                payload_hash=hashlib.sha256(
                    payload_json.encode("utf-8")
                ).hexdigest(),
                payload_json=payload_json,
                disposition="rejected",
                created_by="connector",
            )
            db.add(original)
            db.commit()
            contract_id = contract.id
            original_id = original.id

        paused = client.put(
            "/api/connector-governance/contracts/knowledge-graph-input/pause",
            json={
                "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
                "mechanism_key": "",
                "reason": "上游字段正在调整",
                "expected_revision": None,
            },
        )
        assert paused.status_code == 200, paused.text
        assert paused.json()["state"] == "paused"
        assert paused.json()["revision"] == 1

        blocked_replay = client.post(
            f"/api/connector-governance/intake-events/{original_id}/replay",
            json={"reason": "暂停期间不应绕过接入门禁"},
        )
        assert blocked_replay.status_code == 409, blocked_replay.text
        assert blocked_replay.json()["detail"]["code"] == (
            "upstream_connector_paused"
        )

        with sessions() as db:
            contract = db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, contract_id)
            assert contract is not None
            with pytest.raises(ConnectorIntakeError) as exc_info:
                ingest_upstream_event(
                    db,
                    contract,
                    IntakeRequest(
                        source_event_id="evt-paused",
                        source_cursor="10",
                        source_occurred_at=None,
                        category_key="knowledge_graph",
                        mechanism_key="",
                        payload={"content_id": "asset-paused", "sequence": 10},
                        actor="connector",
                    ),
                )
            assert exc_info.value.code == "upstream_connector_paused"

        resumed = client.put(
            "/api/connector-governance/contracts/knowledge-graph-input/resume",
            json={
                "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
                "mechanism_key": "",
                "reason": "字段检查通过",
                "expected_revision": 1,
            },
        )
        assert resumed.status_code == 200, resumed.text
        assert resumed.json()["state"] == "active"
        assert resumed.json()["revision"] == 2

        replayed = client.post(
            f"/api/connector-governance/intake-events/{original_id}/replay",
            json={"reason": "修复上游字段后重新接入"},
        )
        assert replayed.status_code == 201, replayed.text
        assert replayed.json()["disposition"] == "replayed"
        assert replayed.json()["replay_of_id"] == original_id

        with sessions() as db:
            original = db.get(UpstreamIntakeEvent, original_id)
            replay = db.get(UpstreamIntakeEvent, replayed.json()["id"])
            assert original is not None and replay is not None
            assert original.disposition == "rejected"
            assert original.production_run_id is None
            assert replay.id != original.id
            assert replay.payload_hash == original.payload_hash
            assert replay.payload_json == original.payload_json
            audit = db.scalar(
                select(RuntimeAuditEvent).where(
                    RuntimeAuditEvent.action == "upstream_event_replayed"
                )
            )
            assert audit is not None
            assert "asset-9" not in audit.details_json
            assert json.loads(audit.details_json)["reason"] == (
                "修复上游字段后重新接入"
            )
    finally:
        client.close()


def test_probe_returns_safe_error_without_leaking_connection_details(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    import app.connector_governance_api as module

    client, sessions, role = _client()
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            contract = create_connector_contract_version(
                db,
                created_by="admin",
                **_contract_payload(),
            )
            db.commit()
            contract_id = contract.id

        role["value"] = "admin"

        def fail_probe(_contract):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "mysql://operator:plaintext-password@internal.example/private"
            )

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            module,
            "resolve_configured_connector_poll_adapter",
            fail_probe,
        )
        response = client.post(
            f"/api/connector-governance/contracts/{contract_id}/probe"
        )
        assert response.status_code == 409
        serialized = canonical_json(response.json()).lower()
        assert "plaintext-password" not in serialized
        assert "internal.example" not in serialized
        assert "mysql://" not in serialized
        assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == "connector_probe_failed"
    finally:
        client.close()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_connector_intake.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.connector_contracts import create_connector_contract_version
from app.connector_intake import (
    ConnectorIntakeError,
    FixtureConnectorPollAdapter,
    IntakeRequest,
    PollRecord,
    ingest_upstream_event,
    poll_connector_contract,
)
from app.database import Base
from app.external_datasources import compute_live_schema_fingerprint
from app.models import UpstreamIntakeEvent
from app.readonly_sources import resolve_configured_connector_poll_adapter


def _contract(
    db: Session,
    *,
    out_of_order: str = "delay",
    schema_fingerprint: str = "a" * 64,
):
    return create_connector_contract_version(
        db,
        contract_key=f"knowledge-graph-input-{out_of_order}",
        direction="upstream",
        category_key="knowledge_graph",
        logical_locator="logical.knowledge_graph.input",
        adapter_key="api-json-v1",
        schema_fingerprint=schema_fingerprint,
        field_mapping={"content_id": "content_id"},
        cursor_contract={
            "field": "sequence",
            "kind": "integer",
            "ordering": "ascending",
            "out_of_order": out_of_order,
        },
        idempotency_contract={"field": "event_id"},
        batch_size=100,
        timeout_seconds=30,
        owner="platform",
        status="active",
        created_by="tester",
    )


def _request(**overrides: object) -> IntakeRequest:
    values = {
        "source_event_id": "evt-100",
        "source_cursor": "100",
        "source_occurred_at": datetime(2026, 8, 28, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
        "mechanism_key": "knowledge_graph.evaluation@native-v1",
        "payload": {"content_id": "asset-100", "sequence": 100},
        "actor": "connector-fixture",
    }
    values.update(overrides)
    return IntakeRequest(**values)


def test_identical_event_reuses_fact_and_drifted_payload_is_conflict() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            contract = _contract(db)
            first = ingest_upstream_event(db, contract, _request())
            duplicate = ingest_upstream_event(db, contract, _request())

            assert duplicate.event.id == first.event.id
            assert duplicate.idempotent_replay is True
            assert duplicate.disposition == "accepted"
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(UpstreamIntakeEvent.id))) == 1

            with pytest.raises(
                ConnectorIntakeError,
                match="事件编号已存在但内容不同",
            ) as exc_info:
                ingest_upstream_event(
                    db,
                    contract,
                    replace(
                        _request(),
                        payload={"content_id": "asset-changed", "sequence": 100},
                    ),
                )
            assert exc_info.value.code == "intake_idempotency_conflict"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("policy", "expected"),
    [("delay", "delayed"), ("reject", "rejected"), ("accept", "accepted")],
)
def test_stale_cursor_follows_frozen_contract_policy(
    policy: str,
    expected: str,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            contract = _contract(db, out_of_order=policy)
            ingest_upstream_event(db, contract, _request())
            stale = ingest_upstream_event(
                db,
                contract,
                _request(
                    source_event_id="evt-099",
                    source_cursor="99",
                    payload={"content_id": "asset-099", "sequence": 99},
                ),
            )

            assert stale.disposition == expected
            assert stale.event.disposition == expected
            assert stale.out_of_order is True
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_poll_and_push_share_the_same_intake_path() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            contract = _contract(db)
            adapter = FixtureConnectorPollAdapter(
                read_only=True,
                schema_fingerprint=contract.schema_fingerprint,
                records=(
                    PollRecord(
                        source_event_id="evt-101",
                        source_cursor="101",
                        source_occurred_at=datetime(
                            2026,
                            8,
                            28,
                            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
                        ),
                        payload={"content_id": "asset-101", "sequence": 101},
                    ),
                ),
            )

            result = poll_connector_contract(
                db,
                contract.id,
                actor="poller",
                adapter=adapter,
            )
            replay = ingest_upstream_event(
                db,
                contract,
                _request(
                    source_event_id="evt-101",
                    source_cursor="101",
                    payload={"content_id": "asset-101", "sequence": 101},
                    actor="push-sender",
                ),
            )

            assert result.accepted_count == 1
            assert result.event_ids == (replay.event.id,)
            assert replay.idempotent_replay is True
            assert adapter.fetch_count == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("read_only", "fingerprint", "expected_code"),
    [
        (False, "a" * 64, "upstream_not_read_only"),
        (True, "b" * 64, "upstream_schema_drift"),
    ],
)
def test_poll_fails_closed_before_intake_when_source_is_unsafe(
    read_only: bool,
    fingerprint: str,
    expected_code: str,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            contract = _contract(db)
            adapter = FixtureConnectorPollAdapter(
                read_only=read_only,
                schema_fingerprint=fingerprint,
                records=(),
            )

            with pytest.raises(ConnectorIntakeError) as exc_info:
                poll_connector_contract(
                    db,
                    contract.id,
                    actor="poller",
                    adapter=adapter,
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == expected_code
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(UpstreamIntakeEvent.id))) == 0
            assert adapter.fetch_count == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_configured_sql_poll_resolves_only_deployment_side_physical_details(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    source_path = tmp_path / "upstream.db"
    source_engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{source_path}")
    with source_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE fixture_events ("
            "event_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, sequence_id INTEGER NOT NULL, "
            "occurred_at TEXT NOT NULL, content_id TEXT NOT NULL)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "INSERT INTO fixture_events VALUES "
            "('evt-201', 201, '2026-08-28T04:00:00+00:00', 'asset-201')"
        )
    locator = "logical.knowledge_graph.input"
    monkeypatch.setenv(
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_LOGICAL_KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_INPUT",
        f"sqlite:///{source_path}",
    )
    monkeypatch.setenv(
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_LOGICAL_KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_INPUT_TABLE",
        "fixture_events",
    )
    fingerprint = compute_live_schema_fingerprint(
        locator,
        None,
        table_name="fixture_events",
    )

    app_engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(app_engine)
    try:
        with Session(app_engine) as db:
            contract = create_connector_contract_version(
                db,
                contract_key="knowledge-graph-sql-poll",
                direction="upstream",
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
                logical_locator=locator,
                adapter_key="sql-poll-v1",
                schema_fingerprint=fingerprint,
                field_mapping={
                    "source_event_id": "event_id",
                    "source_cursor": "sequence_id",
                    "source_occurred_at": "occurred_at",
                    "payload": {
                        "content_id": "content_id",
                        "sequence": "sequence_id",
                    },
                },
                cursor_contract={
                    "field": "sequence",
                    "kind": "integer",
                    "ordering": "ascending",
                    "out_of_order": "delay",
                },
                idempotency_contract={"field": "event_id"},
                batch_size=100,
                timeout_seconds=30,
                owner="platform",
                status="active",
                created_by="tester",
            )

            adapter = resolve_configured_connector_poll_adapter(contract)
            result = poll_connector_contract(
                db,
                contract.id,
                actor="sql-poller",
                adapter=adapter,
            )
            event = db.get(UpstreamIntakeEvent, result.event_ids[0])

            assert result.accepted_count == 1
            assert event is not None
            assert event.source_event_id == "evt-201"
            assert event.source_cursor == "201"
            assert "fixture_events" not in contract.logical_locator
            assert "sqlite:" not in contract.logical_locator
    finally:
        app_engine.dispose()
        source_engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_connector_intake_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app import main
from app import connector_intake_api
from app.connector_contracts import create_connector_contract_version
from app.connector_intake import FixtureConnectorPollAdapter, PollRecord
from app.connector_intake_api import (
    connector_continuation_signature,
    connector_push_signature,
)
from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.main import app
from app.models import UpstreamIntakeEvent, User


def _fixture(monkeypatch):
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    admin = User(
        username="connector-admin",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name="连接器管理员",
        is_admin=True,
        role="admin",
    )
    db.add(admin)
    contract = create_connector_contract_version(
        db,
        contract_key="knowledge-graph-input",
        direction="upstream",
        category_key="knowledge_graph",
        logical_locator="logical.knowledge_graph.input",
        adapter_key="api-json-v1",
        schema_fingerprint="a" * 64,
        field_mapping={"content_id": "content_id"},
        cursor_contract={
            "field": "sequence",
            "kind": "integer",
            "ordering": "ascending",
            "out_of_order": "delay",
        },
        idempotency_contract={"field": "event_id"},
        batch_size=100,
        timeout_seconds=30,
        owner="platform",
        status="active",
        created_by="tester",
    )
    db.commit()
    secret = "connector-test-secret-with-enough-entropy"
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        main,
        "settings",
        replace(main.settings, connector_ingress_token=secret),
    )
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
    app.dependency_overrides[main.current_user] = lambda: admin
    return engine, db, TestClient(app), contract, secret


def _payload(*, content_id: str = "asset-100") -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "source_event_id": "evt-100",
        "source_cursor": "100",
        "source_occurred_at": "2026-08-28T04:00:00Z",
        "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
        "mechanism_key": "knowledge_graph.evaluation@native-v1",
        "payload": {"content_id": content_id, "sequence": 100},
    }


def _headers(
    *,
    secret: str,
    payload: dict[str, object],
    timestamp: int,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {
        "X-Connector-Timestamp": str(timestamp),
        "X-Connector-Signature": connector_push_signature(
            secret=secret,
            contract_key="knowledge-graph-input",
            timestamp=str(timestamp),
            source_event_id=str(payload["source_event_id"]),
            payload=payload["payload"],
        ),
    }


def _close(engine, db: Session) -> None:
    app.dependency_overrides.clear()
    db.close()
    engine.dispose()


def test_signed_push_is_idempotent_and_never_echoes_payload_or_secret(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine, db, client, contract, secret = _fixture(monkeypatch)
    now = int(datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp())
    payload = _payload()
    headers = _headers(secret=secret, payload=payload, timestamp=now)
    try:
        first = client.post(
            f"/api/connectors/upstream/{contract.contract_key}/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=payload,
        )
        replay = client.post(
            f"/api/connectors/upstream/{contract.contract_key}/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=payload,
        )

        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        assert replay.status_code == 200, replay.text
        assert first.json()["event_id"] == replay.json()["event_id"]
        assert first.json()["idempotent_replay"] is False
        assert replay.json()["idempotent_replay"] is True
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(UpstreamIntakeEvent.id))) == 1
        assert secret not in first.text
        assert "asset-100" not in first.text
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_push_rejects_stale_timestamp_bad_signature_and_payload_drift(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine, db, client, contract, secret = _fixture(monkeypatch)
    now = int(datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp())
    payload = _payload()
    path = f"/api/connectors/upstream/{contract.contract_key}/events"
    try:
        stale = client.post(
            path,
            headers=_headers(
                secret=secret,
                payload=payload,
                timestamp=now - 301,
            ),
            json=payload,
        )
        bad_signature = client.post(
            path,
            headers={
                "X-Connector-Timestamp": str(now),
                "X-Connector-Signature": "0" * 64,
            },
            json=payload,
        )
        accepted = client.post(
            path,
            headers=_headers(secret=secret, payload=payload, timestamp=now),
            json=payload,
        )
        changed = _payload(content_id="asset-secret-change")
        conflict = client.post(
            path,
            headers=_headers(secret=secret, payload=changed, timestamp=now),
            json=changed,
        )

        assert stale.status_code == 401
        assert bad_signature.status_code == 401
        assert accepted.status_code == 200
        assert conflict.status_code == 409
        assert conflict.json()["detail"]["code"] == "intake_idempotency_conflict"
        assert secret not in conflict.text
        assert "asset-secret-change" not in conflict.text
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_operator_can_poll_a_configured_read_only_contract(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine, db, client, contract, _secret = _fixture(monkeypatch)
    adapter = FixtureConnectorPollAdapter(
        read_only=True,
        schema_fingerprint=contract.schema_fingerprint,
        records=(
            PollRecord(
                source_event_id="evt-poll-1",
                source_cursor="1",
                source_occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
                payload={"content_id": "asset-poll-1", "sequence": 1},
            ),
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        connector_intake_api,
        "resolve_configured_connector_poll_adapter",
        lambda _contract: adapter,
    )
    try:
        response = client.post(
            f"/api/connectors/upstream/{contract.id}/poll"
        )

        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        assert response.json()["accepted_count"] == 1
        assert response.json()["event_count"] == 1
        assert response.json()["contract_id"] == contract.id
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_signed_continuation_callback_ingests_then_resumes_idempotently(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine, db, client, contract, secret = _fixture(monkeypatch)
    correlation_id = "wait_fixture_100"
    now = int(datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp())
    payload = _payload()
    payload["mechanism_key"] = "continuation-fixture"
    resume_calls: list[int] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        connector_intake_api,
        "resolve_external_wait_contract",
        lambda _db, supplied: contract
        if supplied == correlation_id
        else None,
    )

    def resume(_db, request):
        resume_calls.append(request.callback_intake_event_id)
        return SimpleNamespace(id=77, status="queued")

    monkeypatch.setattr(connector_intake_api, "resume_external_wait", resume)

    def headers(body: dict[str, object], timestamp: int = now) -> dict[str, str]:
        return {
            "X-Connector-Timestamp": str(timestamp),
            "X-Connector-Signature": connector_continuation_signature(
                secret=secret,
                correlation_id=correlation_id,
                timestamp=str(timestamp),
                source_event_id=str(body["source_event_id"]),
                payload=body["payload"],
            ),
        }

    path = f"/api/connectors/continuations/{correlation_id}/events"
    try:
        first = client.post(path, headers=headers(payload), json=payload)
        replay = client.post(path, headers=headers(payload), json=payload)

        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        assert replay.status_code == 200, replay.text
        assert first.json()["schema_version"] == (
            "connector-continuation-response-v1"
        )
        assert first.json()["event_id"] == replay.json()["event_id"]
        assert first.json()["contract_version_id"] == contract.id
        assert first.json()["run_id"] == 77
        assert first.json()["run_status"] == "queued"
        assert first.json()["idempotent_replay"] is False
        assert replay.json()["idempotent_replay"] is True
        assert resume_calls == [
            first.json()["event_id"],
            first.json()["event_id"],
        ]
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(UpstreamIntakeEvent.id))) == 1
        assert secret not in first.text
        assert "asset-100" not in first.text

        changed = _payload(content_id="private-content-change")
        changed["mechanism_key"] = "continuation-fixture"
        conflict = client.post(
            path,
            headers=headers(changed),
            json=changed,
        )
        assert conflict.status_code == 409
        assert conflict.json()["detail"]["code"] == (
            "intake_idempotency_conflict"
        )
        assert "private-content-change" not in conflict.text

        stale = client.post(
            path,
            headers=headers(payload, now - 301),
            json=payload,
        )
        assert stale.status_code == 401
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_connector_intake_run_binding.py =====
"""推送路由落库后须把事件绑定成增量运行；类目未启用机制时只标记待运行。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace

from app import connector_intake_api as api
from app.incremental_mechanisms import IncrementalMechanismError
from app.workflow_connector_executor import WorkflowConnectorError


class _Db:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.events: list[str] = []

    def commit(self) -> None:
        self.events.append("commit")

    def rollback(self) -> None:
        self.events.append("rollback")


def test_binds_run_when_mechanism_configured(monkeypatch) -> None:
    import app.incremental_mechanisms as mech
    import app.workflow_connector_executor as executor

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        mech, "resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue",
        lambda db, category_key: SimpleNamespace(workflow_version_id=77),
    )
    seen: dict = {}

    def create(db, *, event_id, workflow_version_id, actor):
        seen.update(event_id=event_id, workflow_version_id=workflow_version_id, actor=actor)
        return SimpleNamespace(id=501, status="queued")

    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "create_incremental_run_from_intake", create)
    db = _Db()
    out = api._bind_incremental_run(db, event_id=9, category_key="inspiration_image", actor="connector:x", disposition="accepted")
    assert out == {"production_run_id": 501, "run_status": "queued"}
    assert seen == {"event_id": 9, "workflow_version_id": 77, "actor": "connector:x"}
    assert db.events == ["commit"]


def test_unconfigured_mechanism_keeps_event_pending(monkeypatch) -> None:
    import app.incremental_mechanisms as mech

    def resolve(db, category_key):
        raise IncrementalMechanismError("incremental_mechanism_not_configured", "未配置")

    monkeypatch.setattr(mech, "resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue", resolve)
    out = api._bind_incremental_run(_Db(), event_id=9, category_key="c", actor="a", disposition="accepted")
    assert out["run_status"] == "mechanism_not_configured"
    assert out["production_run_id"] is None


def test_non_runnable_disposition_is_not_bound() -> None:
    out = api._bind_incremental_run(_Db(), event_id=9, category_key="c", actor="a", disposition="duplicate")
    assert out == {"production_run_id": None, "run_status": "not_runnable"}


def test_binding_failure_rolls_back(monkeypatch) -> None:
    import app.incremental_mechanisms as mech
    import app.workflow_connector_executor as executor

    monkeypatch.setattr(mech, "resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue", lambda db, c: SimpleNamespace(workflow_version_id=1))

    def create(*_a, **_k):
        raise WorkflowConnectorError("upstream_intake_event_not_runnable", "x")

    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "create_incremental_run_from_intake", create)
    db = _Db()
    out = api._bind_incremental_run(db, event_id=9, category_key="c", actor="a", disposition="accepted")
    assert out["run_status"] == "run_binding_failed" and db.events == ["rollback"]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_connector_real_bridge.py =====
import json
import pytest
from dataclasses import replace
from datetime import datetime,timezone
from sqlalchemy import select,func
from app import main
from app.connector_intake_api import connector_push_signature
from app.models import ConnectorContractVersion,ProductionRun,WorkflowModelInvocation,UpstreamIntakeEvent
from app.audit import canonical_json
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture,_close
from test_incremental_autorun import _enable_real_incremental_policy


@pytest.mark.parametrize('scenario',['normal','missing_version','wrong_category','paused','shadow','payload_mode','unsigned_cursor','empty_cursor'])
def test_signed_push_uses_real_authority_and_deduplicates_without_model_io(monkeypatch,scenario):
    engine,db,client,asset=_fixture()
    try:
        version,_=_enable_real_incremental_policy(db,monkeypatch)
        contract=db.scalar(select(ConnectorContractVersion).where(ConnectorContractVersion.direction=='upstream'))
        contract.field_mapping_json=canonical_json({'fields':{'content_id':'source.id','content_version':'source.version','asset_id':'source.asset_id','material_type':'source.material_type'}})
        db.commit()
        secret='test-only-connector-signature'
        monkeypatch.setattr(main,'settings',replace(main.settings,connector_ingress_token=secret))
        payload={'source_event_id':'bridge-1','source_cursor':'1','category_key':'space_image','payload':{'sequence':1,'source':{'id':'one','version':'v1','asset_id':asset.id,'material_type':'全景图'}}}
        if scenario=='missing_version':payload['payload']['source'].pop('version')
        if scenario=='wrong_category':
            fields=json.loads(contract.field_mapping_json);fields['fields']['category_key']='source.category'
            contract.field_mapping_json=canonical_json(fields);payload['payload']['source']['category']='other'
        if scenario=='empty_cursor':payload['source_cursor']=''
        if scenario=='unsigned_cursor':payload['payload'].pop('sequence')
        if scenario=='payload_mode':payload['payload']['model_execution_mode']='simulated'
        if scenario in {'paused','shadow'}:
            from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy,set_admission
            policy=configure_policy(db,category_key='space_image',workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id,
                expected_revision=0,mode='shadow',ratio_percent=100,reason='test',actor='test')
            if scenario=='shadow':set_admission(db,category_key='space_image',workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id,
                expected_revision=policy['revision'],paused=False,reason='test',actor='test')
        db.commit()
        timestamp=str(int(datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()))
        headers={'X-Connector-Timestamp':timestamp,'X-Connector-Signature':connector_push_signature(secret=secret,contract_key=contract.contract_key,timestamp=timestamp,source_event_id='bridge-1',payload=payload['payload'])}
        first=client.post('/api/connectors/upstream/'+contract.contract_key+'/events',json=payload,headers=headers)
        assert first.status_code==200,first.text
        if scenario in {'missing_version','wrong_category','paused','unsigned_cursor','empty_cursor'}:
            assert first.json()['production_run_id'] is None,first.text
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id)))==0
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)))==0
            return
        assert first.json()['production_run_id'],first.text
        run=db.get(ProductionRun,first.json()['production_run_id'])
        assert run.model_execution_mode=='real'
        frozen=json.loads(run.snapshot_json)['runtime_context']
        assert frozen['input_manifest']['material_type']=='全景图'
        if scenario=='shadow':assert frozen['incremental_route']['destination']=='shadow'
        assert frozen['real_execution_budget']['max_cost_micros']>0
        replay=client.post('/api/connectors/upstream/'+contract.contract_key+'/events',json=payload,headers=headers)
        assert replay.status_code==200 and replay.json()['idempotent_replay']
        assert replay.json()['production_run_id']==run.id
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id)))==1
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)))==0
        assert db.scalar(select(UpstreamIntakeEvent)).production_run_id==run.id
    finally:_close(engine,db)

@pytest.mark.parametrize('scenario', ['same_time', 'earlier_time', 'batch', 'scheduled'])
def test_signed_order_and_deferred_backlinks(monkeypatch, scenario):
    from datetime import timedelta
    from app.models import MaterialPackage
    from app.incremental_autorun import dispatch_due_incremental_batches
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        _enable_real_incremental_policy(db, monkeypatch, batch_size=2 if scenario in {'batch', 'scheduled'} else 1)
        contract = db.scalar(select(ConnectorContractVersion).where(ConnectorContractVersion.direction == 'upstream'))
        contract.field_mapping_json = canonical_json({'content_id': 'content_id', 'content_version': 'content_version', 'asset_id': 'asset_id'})
        db.commit()
        secret = 'test-only-connector-signature'
        monkeypatch.setattr(main, 'settings', replace(main.settings, connector_ingress_token=secret))
        def push(sequence, occurred):
            body = {'content_id': str(sequence) if scenario == 'batch' else 'same', 'content_version': str(sequence), 'asset_id': asset.id, 'sequence': sequence}
            event_id = f'order-{sequence}'
            timestamp = str(int(datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()))
            headers = {'X-Connector-Timestamp': timestamp, 'X-Connector-Signature': connector_push_signature(secret=secret, contract_key=contract.contract_key, timestamp=timestamp, source_event_id=event_id, payload=body)}
            response = client.post('/api/connectors/upstream/' + contract.contract_key + '/events', headers=headers, json={'source_event_id': event_id, 'source_cursor': str(sequence), 'source_occurred_at': occurred.isoformat(), 'category_key': 'space_image', 'payload': body})
            assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
            return response.json()
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        first = push(10, now)
        if scenario == 'scheduled':
            package = db.scalar(select(MaterialPackage))
            dispatch_due_incremental_batches(db, actor='test', now=package.created_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc) + timedelta(seconds=3601))
            db.commit()
            expected = 1
        else:
            second = push(11, now - timedelta(seconds=1) if scenario == 'earlier_time' else now)
            assert second['production_run_id'], second
            expected = 2
        db.expire_all()
        events = db.scalars(select(UpstreamIntakeEvent)).all()
        assert len(events) == expected
        assert all(event.production_run_id is not None for event in events)
        assert len({event.production_run_id for event in events}) == expected
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 0
        if scenario in {'same_time', 'earlier_time'}:
            late = push(9, now + timedelta(seconds=1))
            assert late['production_run_id'] is None
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == expected
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_connector_runtime_migration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app import models
from app.connector_contracts import (
    ConnectorContractError,
    create_connector_contract_version,
)
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations import runner as migration_runner


def _contract(**overrides: object):
    values = {
        "contract_key": "knowledge-graph-input",
        "version": 1,
        "direction": "upstream",
        "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
        "logical_locator": "logical.knowledge_graph.input",
        "adapter_key": "api-json-v1",
        "schema_fingerprint": "a" * 64,
        "field_mapping_json": "{}",
        "cursor_contract_json": "{}",
        "idempotency_contract_json": '{"field":"event_id"}',
        "batch_size": 100,
        "timeout_seconds": 30,
        "owner": "platform",
        "status": "active",
        "contract_hash": "b" * 64,
        "created_by": "tester",
    }
    values.update(overrides)
    return models.ConnectorContractVersion(**values)


def _create_contract(db: Session, **overrides: object):
    values = {
        "contract_key": "knowledge-graph-input",
        "direction": "upstream",
        "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
        "logical_locator": "logical.knowledge_graph.input",
        "adapter_key": "api-json-v1",
        "schema_fingerprint": "a" * 64,
        "field_mapping": {"content_id": "content_id"},
        "cursor_contract": {"field": "event_id", "ordering": "ascending"},
        "idempotency_contract": {"field": "event_id"},
        "batch_size": 100,
        "timeout_seconds": 30,
        "owner": "platform",
        "status": "active",
        "created_by": "tester",
    }
    values.update(overrides)
    return create_connector_contract_version(db, **values)


def test_current_schema_records_connector_runtime_tables_and_v83_ledger(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'connector-v83.db'}")
    try:
        Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            migration_runner.run_migrations(connection)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
            ).scalar_one() == max(
                migration.version for migration in migration_runner.MIGRATIONS
            )
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT name FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = 83"
            ).scalar_one() == "add_connector_runtime_contracts"
            assert {
                "connector_contract_versions",
                "upstream_intake_events",
                "connector_pause_states",
            } <= set(inspect(connection).get_table_names())
            outbox_columns = {
                column["name"]
                for column in inspect(connection).get_columns(
                    "runtime_outbox_events"
                )
            }
            assert "target_contract_version_id" in outbox_columns
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_connector_contract_version_and_hash_are_unique() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.add_all([
                _contract(),
                _contract(contract_hash="c" * 64),
            ])
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                db.commit()
            db.rollback()

            db.add_all([
                _contract(),
                _contract(version=2),
            ])
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                db.commit()
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_upstream_event_identity_is_unique_per_contract() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            contract = _contract()
            db.add(contract)
            db.flush()
            common = {
                "contract_version_id": contract.id,
                "source_event_id": "evt-1",
                "source_cursor": "cursor-1",
                "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
                "payload_hash": "d" * 64,
                "payload_json": '{"content_id":"asset-1"}',
                "disposition": "accepted",
            }
            db.add_all([
                models.UpstreamIntakeEvent(**common),
                models.UpstreamIntakeEvent(**common),
            ])
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                db.commit()
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_connector_pause_scope_is_unique_and_state_is_checked() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            common = {
                "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
                "mechanism_key": "knowledge_graph.evaluation@native-v1",
                "target_contract_key": "knowledge-graph-output",
                "state": "paused",
                "reason_code": "human_review_severe_overflow",
                "paused_by": "tester",
            }
            db.add_all([
                models.ConnectorPauseState(**common),
                models.ConnectorPauseState(**common),
            ])
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                db.commit()
            db.rollback()

            db.add(models.ConnectorPauseState(**{**common, "state": "unknown"}))
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                db.commit()
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("overrides", "expected_message"),
    [
        (
            {"logical_locator": "mysql+pymysql://user:pass@example/db"},
            "逻辑引用",
        ),
        (
            {"logical_locator": "production.actual_target_table"},
            "逻辑引用",
        ),
        (
            {"field_mapping": {"content_id": "content_id", "password": "x"}},
            "敏感配置",
        ),
        (
            {"cursor_contract": {"sql": "SELECT * FROM target"}},
            "敏感配置",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_connector_contract_rejects_physical_or_secret_configuration(
    overrides: dict[str, object],
    expected_message: str,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            with pytest.raises(ConnectorContractError, match=expected_message):
                _create_contract(db, **overrides)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_connector_contract_is_content_idempotent_and_versions_changes() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            first = _create_contract(db)
            duplicate = _create_contract(db)
            changed = _create_contract(db, timeout_seconds=45)

            assert duplicate.id == first.id
            assert duplicate.version == 1
            assert changed.version == 2
            assert changed.contract_hash != first.contract_hash
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_content_ingress_incremental_routing.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import json

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app import main
from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.incremental_mechanisms import set_incremental_mechanism
from app.main import app
from app.migrations import run_migrations
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    AuditEvent,
    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding,
    ContentIngressEvent,
    MaterialPackage,
    MaterialPackageItem,
    ProductionRun,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions


def _fixture(*, active_mechanism: bool = True) -> tuple[object, Session, TestClient, Asset]:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="asset-1.jpg",
        stored_name="asset-1.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="a" * 64,
        category_key="space_image",
    )
    db.add(asset)
    if active_mechanism:
        versions = seed_incremental_versions(db)
        set_incremental_mechanism(
            db,
            category_key="space_image",
            workflow_version_id=versions[0].id,
            expected_lock_version=None,
            reason="接入测试启用",
            actor="test",
        )
    db.commit()
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
    return engine, db, TestClient(app), asset


def _payload(*, event_id: str, asset_id: int | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    payload: dict[str, object] = {
        "content_id": "asset-1",
        "content_version": "v1",
        "category_key": "space_image",
    }
    if asset_id is not None:
        payload["asset_id"] = asset_id
    return {
        "event_id": event_id,
        "schema_version": "content-ingress-v1",
        "event_type": "content.created",
        "source_system": "upstream-sim",
        "occurred_at": datetime(2026, 8, 13, tzinfo=timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "payload": payload,
    }


def _headers(monkeypatch) -> dict[str, str]:
    token = "test-content-ingress-token"
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        main,
        "settings",
        replace(main.settings, content_ingress_token=token),
    )
    return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}


def _close(engine: object, db: Session) -> None:
    app.dependency_overrides.clear()
    db.close()
    engine.dispose()


def test_content_created_builds_incremental_package(monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        response = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=_headers(monkeypatch),
            json=_payload(event_id="evt-1", asset_id=asset.id),
        )

        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        body = response.json()
        assert body["workflow_kind"] == "incremental"
        assert body["routing_status"] == "dispatched"
        assert body["content"]["content_key"] == "upstream-sim:asset-1"
        assert body["package_created"] is True
        assert body["writes_evaluation_job"] is True
        assert body["production_run_key"].startswith("run-")
        package = db.get(MaterialPackage, body["material_package_id"])
        assert package is not None
        assert package.source == "production_import"
        assert package.category_key == "space_image"
        assert package.workflow_version_id is not None
        mechanism_snapshot = json.loads(package.mechanism_snapshot_json)
        assert mechanism_snapshot["workflow_version_id"] == package.workflow_version_id
        assert mechanism_snapshot["binding_id"]
        assert len(mechanism_snapshot["snapshot_hash"]) == 64
        assert mechanism_snapshot["workflow_snapshot"]["runtime_context"][
            "run_mode"
        ] == "incremental"
        item = db.scalar(
            select(MaterialPackageItem).where(
                MaterialPackageItem.package_id == package.id
            )
        )
        assert item is not None
        assert item.asset_id == asset.id
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_duplicate_content_event_reuses_incremental_package(monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    payload = _payload(event_id="evt-duplicate", asset_id=asset.id)
    try:
        first = client.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=headers, json=payload)
        first_package = db.get(MaterialPackage, first.json()["material_package_id"])
        assert first_package is not None
        first_workflow_version_id = first_package.workflow_version_id
        binding = db.scalar(
            select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding).where(
                CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.category_key == "space_image"
            )
        )
        versions = db.scalars(
            select(WorkflowVersion).order_by(WorkflowVersion.id.asc())
        ).all()
        assert binding is not None and len(versions) == 2
        set_incremental_mechanism(
            db,
            category_key="space_image",
            workflow_version_id=versions[1].id,
            expected_lock_version=binding.lock_version,
            reason="重复事件到达前切换机制",
            actor="test",
        )
        db.commit()

        second = client.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=headers, json=payload)

        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        assert second.status_code == 200, second.text
        assert second.json()["duplicate"] is True
        assert second.json()["package_created"] is False
        assert second.json()["material_package_id"] == first.json()["material_package_id"]
        reused = db.get(MaterialPackage, second.json()["material_package_id"])
        assert reused is not None
        assert reused.workflow_version_id == first_workflow_version_id
        assert json.loads(reused.mechanism_snapshot_json)["workflow_version_id"] == (
            first_workflow_version_id
        )
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(MaterialPackage.id))) == 1
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(MaterialPackageItem.id))) == 1
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count(AuditEvent.id)).where(
                AuditEvent.category == "content_ingress",
                AuditEvent.action == "duplicate_reused",
            )
        ) == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_content_without_local_asset_waits_without_package(monkeypatch) -> None:

        assert body["routing_status"] == "awaiting_material"
        assert body["material_package_id"] is None
        assert body["package_created"] is False
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(MaterialPackage.id))) == 0
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

def test_legacy_duplicate_without_event_asset_evidence_cannot_bind_latest_asset(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """E1(v1/A1) replay after E2(v2/A2) must never authorize v1 with A2."""

    engine, db, client, asset_v1 = _fixture(active_mechanism=False)
    versions = seed_incremental_versions(db)
    db.commit()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    try:
        seed_payload = _payload(event_id="evt-seed-v0", asset_id=asset_v1.id)
        seed_payload["payload"]["content_version"] = "v0"
        seed_payload["occurred_at"] = datetime(
            2026, 8, 13, 0, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc
        ).isoformat()
        assert client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events", headers=headers, json=seed_payload
        ).json()["routing_status"] == "blocked_profile"

        event_v1_payload = _payload(event_id="evt-legacy-v1", asset_id=None)
        event_v1_payload["event_type"] = "content.updated"
        event_v1_payload["occurred_at"] = datetime(
            2026, 8, 13, 0, 0, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc
        ).isoformat()
        record = db.scalar(
            select(ContentIngressEvent).where(
                ContentIngressEvent.event_id == "evt-seed-v0"
            )
        )
        assert record is not None and record.content_record_id is not None
        legacy_payload = event_v1_payload["payload"]
        legacy_event = ContentIngressEvent(
            event_id="evt-legacy-v1",
            schema_version="content-ingress-v1",
            event_type="content.updated",
            source_system="upstream-sim",
            occurred_at=datetime(2026, 8, 13, 0, 0, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
            payload_hash=hashlib.sha256(
                canonical_json(legacy_payload).encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest(),
            payload_json=canonical_json(legacy_payload),
            identity_snapshot_json=None,
            content_record_id=record.content_record_id,
            status="applied",
            received_by="legacy-import",
        )
        db.add(legacy_event)

        asset_v2 = Asset(
            original_name="asset-1-v2.jpg",
            stored_name="asset-1-v2.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=20,
            sha256="b" * 64,
            category_key="space_image",
        )
        db.add(asset_v2)
        db.commit()
        event_v2_payload = _payload(
            event_id="evt-current-v2",
            asset_id=asset_v2.id,
        )
        event_v2_payload["event_type"] = "content.updated"
        event_v2_payload["payload"]["content_version"] = "v2"
        event_v2_payload["occurred_at"] = datetime(
            2026, 8, 13, 0, 0, 2, tzinfo=timezone.utc
        ).isoformat()
        assert client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events", headers=headers, json=event_v2_payload
        ).json()["routing_status"] == "blocked_profile"

        set_incremental_mechanism(
            db,
            category_key="space_image",
            workflow_version_id=versions[0].id,
            expected_lock_version=None,
            reason="启用重放安全回归",
            actor="test",
        )
        db.commit()

        replay = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=event_v1_payload,
        )

        assert replay.status_code == 200, replay.text
        assert replay.json()["duplicate"] is True
        assert replay.json()["routing_status"] == "blocked_input"
        assert replay.json()["material_package_id"] is None
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(MaterialPackage.id))) == 0
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 0
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count(AuditEvent.id)).where(
                AuditEvent.category == "content_ingress",
                AuditEvent.action == "blocked_input",
            )
        ) == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app import main
from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.incremental_mechanisms import set_incremental_mechanism
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.migrations import run_migrations
from app.model_3d_su_category_seed import seed_model_3d_su
from app.models import Asset, ModelConfig, User
from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions


def _v2_payload(
    *,
    event_id: str,
    asset_id: int,
    res_type: int = 1,
    ll_id: str = "12345",
) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "event_id": event_id,
        "schema_version": "content-ingress-v2",
        "event_type": "content.created",
        "source_system": "aliyun_3d66_dw",
        "occurred_at": datetime(2026, 8, 18, tzinfo=timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "payload": {
            "content_id": f"{res_type}:{ll_id}",
            "content_version": "2026-08-18",
            "category_key": "model_3d_su",
            "asset_id": asset_id,
            "res_type": res_type,
            "ll_id": ll_id,
            "res_id": f"res-{ll_id}",
        },
    }


def _verification_payload(*, result: str = "verified") -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "contract_key": "semantic-platform",
        "source_system": "aliyun_3d66_dw",
        "key_fields": ["res_type", "ll_id"],
        "result": result,
        "probe_hash": "a" * 64,
        "data_window": "2026-08-01/2026-08-15",
        "scoped_row_count": 100,
        "duplicate_key_count": 0 if result == "verified" else 1,
        "res_id_conflict_count": 0,
    }


def _create_v2_contract(client: TestClient) -> dict[str, object]:
    from tests.test_semantic_tag_contracts import valid_contract_v2

    response = client.post(
        "/api/tag-demand-contracts",
        json={
            "contract_key": "semantic-platform",
            "definition": valid_contract_v2(),
            "status": "draft",
        },
    )
    assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
    return response.json()


def _approve_identity(client: TestClient) -> dict[str, object]:
    _create_v2_contract(client)
    created = client.post(
        "/api/source-identity-verifications",
        json=_verification_payload(),
    )
    assert created.status_code == 201, created.text
    approved = client.post(
        f"/api/source-identity-verifications/{created.json()['id']}/approve"
    )
    assert approved.status_code == 200, approved.text
    return approved.json()


def _fixture_model_3d_su() -> tuple[object, Session, TestClient, Asset]:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    admin = User(
        username="identity-admin",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name="身份管理员",
        is_admin=True,
        role="admin",
    )
    db.add_all([admin, ModelConfig(active=True)])
    db.commit()
    seed_model_3d_su(db, main.settings)
    workflow_version = seed_incremental_versions(
        db,
        category_key="model_3d_su",
        count=1,
    )[0]
    set_incremental_mechanism(
        db,
        category_key="model_3d_su",
        workflow_version_id=workflow_version.id,
        expected_lock_version=None,
        reason="身份接入测试启用",
        actor="test",
    )
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="model-3d-su.jpg",
        stored_name="model-3d-su.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256="b" * 64,
        category_key="model_3d_su",
    )
    db.add(asset)
    db.commit()
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: admin
    return engine, db, TestClient(app), asset

        response = client.post(

def test_conflict_probe_cannot_be_approved_as_verified() -> None:
    engine, db, client, _asset = _fixture_model_3d_su()

        _create_v2_contract(client)
        created = client.post(
            "/api/source-identity-verifications",
            json=_verification_payload(result="conflict"),
        )
        assert created.status_code == 201, created.text
        response = client.post(
