    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_same_v2_event_id_with_changed_identity_returns_409(monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture_model_3d_su()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    try:
        _approve_identity(client)
        first_payload = _v2_payload(
            event_id="evt-identity-drift",
            asset_id=asset.id,
            res_type=1,
            ll_id="12345",
        )
        second_payload = _v2_payload(
            event_id="evt-identity-drift",
            asset_id=asset.id,
            res_type=1,
            ll_id="99999",
        )
        first = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events", headers=headers, json=first_payload
        )
        second = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events", headers=headers, json=second_payload
        )
        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        assert second.status_code == 409
        assert second.json()["detail"]["code"] == "INGRESS_EVENT_CONFLICT"
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_continuation_submission_wait.py =====
"""B-stage continuations must survive transport wrappers and advisory pacing."""
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models import ModelConfig, ModelQuotaPool, ProductionStepAttempt
from app.model_quota import ModelQuotaUnavailable
from test_batch_fallback_execution_integration import fallback_case
from test_batch_submission_pacing import database, admit, NOW


def test_foreach_child_transport_keeps_existing_continuation_class():
    from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import invocation_prefix
    from app.batch_submission_fairness import is_continuation
    assert is_continuation(node={'config': {'asset_key': 'prompt_a'}}, attempt_no=1,
        invocation_key=invocation_prefix('foreach:child:7') + 'batch:1')


@pytest.mark.parametrize('asset,key_suffix,attempt_no,expected', [
    ('prompt_a', 'batch:1', 1, False),
    ('prompt_b', 'batch:1', 1, True),
    ('prompt_a', 'batch:1:reservation:2', 1, True),
    ('prompt_a', 'batch:2', 1, True),
    ('prompt_a', 'online:1', 1, True),
    ('prompt_a', 'batch:1', 2, True),
])
def test_real_transport_ticket_keeps_new_a_distinct_from_continuation(
        fallback_case, monkeypatch, asset, key_suffix, attempt_no, expected):
    from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import invocation_prefix
    from app.batch_submission_fairness import register_ready, KEY
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_FAIR_SUBMISSION', '1')
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='continuation', name='continuation', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='fixture', created_by='test', max_concurrency=8)
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id'])
        attempt.attempt_no = attempt_no
        db.commit()
        node = {**kwargs['node'], 'config': {**kwargs['node']['config'], 'asset_key': asset}}
        register_ready(db, attempt_id=attempt.id, lease_token=kwargs['lease_token'],
            worker_id=kwargs['worker_id'], node=node,
            invocation_key=invocation_prefix('root') + key_suffix)
        checkpoint = json.loads(attempt.checkpoint_json)
        assert checkpoint[KEY]['continuation'] is expected


@pytest.mark.parametrize('continuation,maximum', [(False, 644), (True, 104)])
def test_continuation_caps_only_extra_spread_not_authoritative_spacing(
        database, monkeypatch, continuation, maximum):
    from app.batch_submission import check_submission
    monkeypatch.setattr('app.batch_submission.random.uniform', lambda low, high: high)
    engine, pool_id = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool_id, 1, NOW)
        pool = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool_id)
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
            check_submission(db, pool=pool, reserved_tokens=0,
                now=NOW + timedelta(seconds=1), waiting_hint=253, continuation=continuation)
        assert caught.value.retry_after_seconds == maximum
        assert caught.value.code == 'model_batch_submission_wait'


def test_continuation_keeps_provider_retry_after(database, monkeypatch):
    from app.batch_submission import check_submission
    monkeypatch.setattr('app.batch_submission.random.uniform', lambda low, high: high)
    monkeypatch.setattr('app.batch_submission._feedback', lambda *args: {
        'retry_at': NOW.timestamp() + 900})
    engine, pool_id = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        pool = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool_id)
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
            check_submission(db, pool=pool, reserved_tokens=0, now=NOW,
                waiting_hint=253, continuation=True)
        assert caught.value.retry_after_seconds == 960


@pytest.mark.parametrize('asset,expected', [('prompt_a', False), ('prompt_b', True)])
def test_preflight_routes_actual_node_to_continuation_pacing(fallback_case, monkeypatch, asset, expected):
    from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import invocation_prefix
    from app.runtime_admission import preflight_model_pool
    from app.models import ProductionRun
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    seen = []
    monkeypatch.setattr('app.batch_submission.check_submission',
        lambda db, **values: seen.append(values))
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='preflight-continuation', name='preflight', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='fixture', created_by='test', max_concurrency=8)
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        db.commit()
        attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id'])
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, attempt.run_id)
        node = {**kwargs['node'], 'config': {**kwargs['node']['config'], 'asset_key': asset}}
        preflight_model_pool(db, run=run, attempt=attempt,
            lease_token=kwargs['lease_token'], worker_id=kwargs['worker_id'], node=node,
            transport_mode='batch', invocation_key=invocation_prefix('root') + 'batch:1')
        assert len(seen) == 1 and seen[0]['continuation'] is expected
        assert seen[0]['waiting_hint'] == 0

===== FILE backend/tests/test_contract_scoring_capabilities.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from app.category_evaluation_contract import (
    CATEGORY_EVALUATION_CONTRACT_VERSION,
    resolve_scoring_capabilities,
)
from app.dimension_deduction_bridge import rule_scoring_mode
from app.redline_policy import REDLINE_POLICY_FORMAT_VERSION


def _contract() -> dict:
    return {
        "schema_version": CATEGORY_EVALUATION_CONTRACT_VERSION,
        "category_key": "operator_custom",
        "level_scale": {
            "version": "category-level-scale-v1",
            "levels": [
                {"level": "L1", "enabled": True, "min_score": 80},
                {"level": "L2", "enabled": True, "min_score": 60},
                {"level": "L3", "enabled": True, "min_score": 40},
                {"level": "L4", "enabled": True, "min_score": 0},
                {"level": "L5", "enabled": False},
            ],
        },
        "redline_policy": {
            "format_version": REDLINE_POLICY_FORMAT_VERSION,
            "enabled": False,
            "hit_level": "L4",
            "hit_score_cap": 40,
            "rules": [],
        },
        "track_classification": {
            "format_version": "track-classification-v1",
            "default_track": "class_one",
            "tracks": [
                {
                    "key": "class_one",
                    "label": "一类",
                    "base_score": 40,
                    "dimension_max": 60,
                    "track_cap": 100,
                    "dimension_schema_ref": {"schema_key": "custom", "version": "1"},
                },
                {
                    "key": "class_two",
                    "label": "二类",
                    "base_score": 20,
                    "dimension_max": 60,
                    "track_cap": 80,
                    "dimension_schema_ref": {"schema_key": "custom", "version": "1"},
                },
            ],
        },
        "common_modifiers": {
            "format_version": "common-modifiers-v1",
            "media_type_penalty": {
                "enabled": True,
                "baseline": "real_photo",
                "penalties": {
                    "real_photo": 0,
                    "render_3d": -5,
                    "ai_image": -10,
                    "other": -10,
                },
            },
            "high_score_veto": {"enabled": False, "threshold": 80, "cap_to": 79},
            "hard_defect_penalty": {
                "enabled": True,
                "per_hit": 10,
                "source": "hard_defects",
            },
        },
        "track_adjustments": {"class_two": {"deduction": 20}},
    }


def _dimensions(*, with_rules: bool) -> dict:
    dimension = {
        "key": "composition",
        "label": "构图",
        "weight": 1.0,
        "grade_points": {"1": 0.0, "2": 25.0, "3": 50.0, "4": 75.0, "5": 100.0},
    }
    if with_rules:
        dimension["deduction_rules"] = [
            {
                "rule_id": "minor_defect",
                "description": "存在轻微构图缺陷",
                "deduction": 20,
                "tags": [],
            }
        ]
    return {
        "format_version": "subcategory-dimensions-v1",
        "sub_category_key": "class_one",
        "dimension_max": 60,
        "grade_output_contract": {
            "format_version": "dimension-grade-output-v1",
            "require_exact_keys": True,
            "evidence_required": True,
        },
        "common_group": {
            "group_weight": 1.0,
            "schema_definition": {
                "format_version": "dimension-schema-definition-v1",
                "schema_key": "custom",
                "version": "1",
                "dimensions": [dimension],
            },
        },
        "specific_group": {"schema_definition": {"dimensions": []}},
    }


def test_explicit_dimension_rules_override_static_grade_marker() -> None:
    assert rule_scoring_mode(_dimensions(with_rules=True)) == "deduction_v1"


def test_capability_resolution_lists_all_declared_executable_primitives() -> None:
    capabilities = resolve_scoring_capabilities(
        _contract(), {"class_one": _dimensions(with_rules=True)}
    )

    assert capabilities["execution_mode"] == "rule_deduction"
    assert capabilities["primitives"] == [
        "redline",
        "track_adjustment",
        "dimension_rule_deduction",
        "media_penalty",
        "hard_defect_penalty",
        "level_scale",
    ]


def test_capability_resolution_keeps_grade_fallback_for_legacy_dimension() -> None:
    capabilities = resolve_scoring_capabilities(
        _contract(), {"class_one": _dimensions(with_rules=False)}
    )

    assert capabilities["execution_mode"] == "grade_fallback"
    assert "dimension_rule_deduction" not in capabilities["primitives"]


def test_capability_resolution_is_per_track_when_tracks_use_different_modes() -> None:
    legacy = _dimensions(with_rules=False)
    rule_based = _dimensions(with_rules=True)
    capabilities = resolve_scoring_capabilities(
        _contract(),
        {"class_one": rule_based, "class_two": legacy},
    )

    assert capabilities["execution_mode"] == "per_track"
    assert capabilities["track_modes"] == {
        "class_one": "rule_deduction",
        "class_two": "grade_fallback",
    }
    assert "dimension_rule_deduction" in capabilities["primitives"]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_correction_contract.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json

import pytest

from app.correction_contract import (
    ContractValidationError,
    assert_correction_contract_complete,
    correction_fields_from_output_contract,
    correction_contract_hash,
    freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot,
    inherit_correction_node,
    normalize_correction_contract,
    validate_node_value,
)


def test_normalize_contract_sorts_nodes_and_hash_is_stable() -> None:
    raw = {
        "contract_version": "1",
        "category_key": "inspiration_image",
        "nodes": [
            {
                "node_key": "b",
                "layer": "B",
                "label": "字段B",
                "type": "text",
                "semantic_version": "1",
            },
            {
                "node_key": "a",
                "layer": "A",
                "label": "字段A",
                "type": "enum",
                "options": ["x"],
                "semantic_version": "1",
            },
        ],
    }

    normalized = normalize_correction_contract(raw, category_key="inspiration_image")

    assert [node["node_key"] for node in normalized["nodes"]] == ["a", "b"]
    assert correction_contract_hash(normalized) == correction_contract_hash(normalized.copy())

    with_hash = {**normalized, "contract_hash": "ignored-by-hash"}
    assert correction_contract_hash(with_hash) == correction_contract_hash(normalized)


def test_normalize_contract_keeps_unknown_node_metadata() -> None:
    normalized = normalize_correction_contract(
        {
            "contract_version": "1",
            "category_key": "inspiration_image",
            "nodes": [
                {
                    "node_key": "a",
                    "layer": "A",
                    "path": "source",
                    "order": 2,
                    "label": "字段A",
                    "type": "text",
                    "semantic_version": "1",
                    "future_flag": True,
                }
            ],
        },
        category_key="inspiration_image",
    )

    assert normalized["nodes"][0]["metadata"] == {"future_flag": True}


def test_validate_complete_contract_requires_v3_recompute_reference() -> None:
    contract = {
        "contract_version": "1",
        "category_key": "inspiration_image",
        "nodes": [
            {
                "node_key": "v3.final",
                "layer": "V3",
                "path": "final",
                "order": 1,
                "label": "最终等级",
                "description": "根据规则确定最终等级",
                "type": "enum",
                "options": ["L1", "L2"],
                "semantic_version": "1",
                "compatibility_key": "final-level",
                "required": True,
                "evidence": {"description": "需要边界证据"},
            }
        ],
    }

    errors = []
    from app.correction_contract import validate_correction_contract

    errors = validate_correction_contract(contract)

    assert any("recompute_ref" in error for error in errors)
    with pytest.raises(ContractValidationError) as exc_info:
        assert_correction_contract_complete(contract)
    assert exc_info.value.code == "CORRECTION_CONTRACT_INCOMPLETE"
    assert "v3.final" in exc_info.value.fields


def test_inherit_only_compatible_stable_node() -> None:
    previous = {
        "node_key": "v3.final",
        "type": "enum",
        "semantic_version": "2",
        "compatibility_key": "final-level",
        "human_value": "L2",
        "reason": "证据",
        "evidence": [{"text": "边界"}],
    }
    current = {
        "node_key": "v3.final",
        "type": "enum",
        "semantic_version": "2",
        "compatibility_key": "final-level",
    }

    inherited = inherit_correction_node(previous, current)

    assert inherited["inheritance"]["status"] == "inherited"
    assert inherited["human_value"] == "L2"
    assert inherited["reason"] == "证据"


def test_semantic_change_does_not_inherit() -> None:
    previous = {
        "node_key": "v3.final",
        "type": "enum",
        "semantic_version": "1",
        "compatibility_key": "old",
        "human_value": "L2",
    }
    current = {
        "node_key": "v3.final",
        "type": "enum",
        "semantic_version": "2",
        "compatibility_key": "new",
    }

    result = inherit_correction_node(previous, current)

    assert result["inheritance"]["status"] == "changed"
    assert "human_value" not in result


def test_validate_node_value_rejects_value_outside_enum() -> None:
    node = {"node_key": "a", "type": "enum", "options": ["yes", "no"]}

    with pytest.raises(ContractValidationError) as exc_info:
        validate_node_value(node, "maybe")

    assert exc_info.value.code == "CORRECTION_NODE_VALUE_INVALID"
    assert exc_info.value.fields == ["a"]


def test_complete_contract_rejects_unknown_node_type_but_accepts_rule_hit() -> None:
    base = {
        "contract_version": "1",
        "category_key": "inspiration_image",
        "nodes": [
            {
                "node_key": "v3.rule",
                "layer": "V3",
                "path": "dimension.composition.hit_rules.r1",
                "order": 1,
                "label": "构图规则",
                "description": "人工判断构图规则是否命中",
                "type": "unknown_widget",
                "semantic_version": "1",
                "compatibility_key": "composition-r1",
                "required": True,
                "evidence": {"description": "需要图片证据"},
                "recompute_ref": "evaluation_v3_pipeline.recompute_qualified_v3",
            }
        ],
    }

    with pytest.raises(ContractValidationError) as exc_info:
        assert_correction_contract_complete(base)
    assert "type" in str(exc_info.value)

    base["nodes"][0]["type"] = "rule_hit"
    assert_correction_contract_complete(base)

def test_strict_prompt_contract_derives_dynamic_correction_fields() -> None:
    """Catches prompt A field additions being hidden from correction operators."""

    output_contract = {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": "unified_aesthetic",
        "description": "一次调用输出完整美感事实",
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "composition": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "title": "构图",
                    "description": "构图判断",
                },
                "color": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "title": "色彩",
                    "description": "色彩判断",
                },
                "evidence": {
                    "type": "array",
                    "title": "证据",
                    "description": "逐项视觉证据",
                    "items": {"type": "string"},
                },
            },
            "required": ["composition", "color", "evidence"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }
    result = {
        "composition": "主体居中",
        "color": "冷暖平衡",
        "evidence": ["主体边缘清晰"],
    }

    fields = correction_fields_from_output_contract(output_contract, result)

    assert [field["key"] for field in fields] == [
        "composition",
        "color",
        "evidence",
    ]
    assert [field["label"] for field in fields] == ["构图", "色彩", "证据"]
    assert all(field["required"] is True for field in fields)
    assert fields[2]["value"] == ["主体边缘清晰"]


def test_workflow_contract_uses_visual_prompt_schema_even_when_node_schema_is_legacy() -> None:
    """Catches config.output_contract being ignored by the frozen correction form."""

    output_contract = {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": "unified_aesthetic",
        "description": "一次调用输出完整美感事实",
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "composition": {"type": "string", "title": "构图"},
                "color": {"type": "string", "title": "色彩"},
                "evidence": {
                    "type": "array",
                    "title": "证据",
                    "items": {"type": "string"},
                },
            },
            "required": ["composition", "color", "evidence"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }
    snapshot = {
        "workflow": {
            "workflow_definition_id": 1,
            "workflow_version_id": 2,
            "version": "3",
            "canonical_hash": "c" * 64,
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "category_key": "inspiration_image",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "prompt_unified",
                        "kind": "prompt",
                        "display_name": "一次提示词",
                        "config": {"output_contract": output_contract},
                        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                    }
                ],
                "edges": [],
            },
        }
    }

    contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(snapshot)

    prompt_nodes = [
        node
        for node in contract["nodes"]
        if node["node_key"].startswith("workflow.prompt_unified.")
    ]
    assert {node["node_key"] for node in prompt_nodes} == {
        "workflow.prompt_unified.composition",
        "workflow.prompt_unified.color",
        "workflow.prompt_unified.evidence",
    }
    assert all(node["required"] is True for node in prompt_nodes)


def test_nested_strict_schema_derives_scalar_and_array_correction_leaves() -> None:
    """Nested fixed fields are editable without collapsing to opaque JSON."""

    output_contract = {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": "nested_aesthetic",
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "dimensions": {
                    "type": "object",
                    "properties": {
                        "color_material": {
                            "type": "object",
                            "properties": {
                                "grade": {
                                    "type": "integer",
                                    "minimum": 1,
                                    "maximum": 5,
                                    "title": "色彩材质等级",
                                },
                                "evidence": {
                                    "type": "array",
                                    "items": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
                                    "title": "色彩材质证据",
                                },
                            },
                            "required": ["grade", "evidence"],
                            "additionalProperties": False,
                        }
                    },
                    "required": ["color_material"],
                    "additionalProperties": False,
                },
                "labels": {
                    "type": "array",
                    "items": {"type": "string", "enum": ["柔和", "清晰"]},
                    "title": "动态标签",
                },
            },
            "required": ["dimensions", "labels"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }
    result = {
        "dimensions": {
            "color_material": {
                "grade": 4,
                "evidence": ["木饰面颜色自然"],
            }
        },
        "labels": ["柔和"],
    }

    fields = correction_fields_from_output_contract(output_contract, result)

    assert [field["key"] for field in fields] == [
        "dimensions.color_material.grade",
        "dimensions.color_material.evidence",
        "labels",
    ]
    assert fields[0]["value"] == 4
    assert fields[0]["minimum"] == 1
    assert fields[1]["value"] == ["木饰面颜色自然"]
    assert fields[2]["value"] == ["柔和"]
    assert fields[2]["item_schema"] == {
        "type": "string",
        "enum": ["柔和", "清晰"],
    }
    frozen = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(
        {
            "workflow": {
                "workflow_definition_id": 1,
                "workflow_version_id": 2,
                "version": "3",
                "canonical_hash": "d" * 64,
                "manifest": {
                    "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                    "category_key": "inspiration_image",
                    "nodes": [
                        {
                            "node_instance_id": "prompt_nested",
                            "kind": "prompt",
                            "config": {"output_contract": output_contract},
                        }
                    ],
                    "edges": [],
                },
            }
        }
    )
    frozen_by_key = {node["node_key"]: node for node in frozen["nodes"]}
    assert "workflow.prompt_nested.dimensions.color_material.grade" in frozen_by_key
    label_node = frozen_by_key["workflow.prompt_nested.labels"]
    assert label_node["metadata"]["output_array_item_schema"] == {
        "type": "string",
        "enum": ["柔和", "清晰"],
    }


def test_each_historical_run_uses_only_its_frozen_prompt_schema_fields() -> None:
    old_contract = {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": "old_output",
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "score": {"type": "integer"},
                "reason": {"type": "string"},
            },
            "required": ["score", "reason"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }
    new_contract = {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": "new_output",
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "score": {"type": "integer"},
                "labels": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
            },
            "required": ["score", "labels"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }

    assert [
        field["key"]
        for field in correction_fields_from_output_contract(old_contract, {})
    ] == ["score", "reason"]
    assert [
        field["key"]
        for field in correction_fields_from_output_contract(new_contract, {})
    ] == ["score", "labels"]


def test_const_output_fields_are_not_exposed_as_operator_corrections() -> None:
    contract = {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": "case_final_score",
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "score": {"type": "integer"},
                "side_effect_executed": {"type": "boolean", "const": False},
                "deletion_mode": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "const": "recommend_only",
                },
            },
            "required": ["score", "side_effect_executed", "deletion_mode"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }

    fields = correction_fields_from_output_contract(
        contract,
        {
            "score": 82,
            "side_effect_executed": False,
            "deletion_mode": "recommend_only",
        },
    )

    assert [field["key"] for field in fields] == ["score"]


def _strict_contract(name: str, properties: dict) -> dict:
    return {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": name,
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": properties,
            "required": list(properties),
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }


def test_frozen_contract_discovers_three_prompts_foreach_and_case_fields() -> None:
    prompt_a = _strict_contract(
        "prompt_a",
        {"scope": {"type": "string", "title": "范围"}},
    )
    prompt_b = _strict_contract(
        "prompt_b",
        {"aesthetic_score": {"type": "integer", "title": "美感分"}},
    )
    prompt_c = _strict_contract(
        "prompt_c",
        {"tags": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "title": "标签"}},
    )
    item_contract = _strict_contract(
        "case_image",
        {
            "score": {"type": "integer", "title": "图片分"},
            "details": {
                "type": "object",
                "title": "图片细节",
                "properties": {
                    "style": {"type": "string", "title": "风格"},
                },
                "required": ["style"],
                "additionalProperties": False,
            },
        },
    )
    aggregate_schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "score": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "mean": {"type": "number", "title": "图片均分"},
                },
                "required": ["mean"],
                "additionalProperties": False,
            }
        },
        "required": ["score"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
    }
    snapshot = {
        "workflow": {
            "workflow_definition_id": 11,
            "workflow_version_id": 12,
            "version": "7",
            "canonical_hash": "e" * 64,
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "category_key": "design_case",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "prompt_a",
                        "kind": "prompt",
                        "config": {"output_contract": prompt_a},
                    },
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "prompt_b",
                        "kind": "prompt",
                        "config": {"output_contract": prompt_b},
                    },
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "prompt_c",
                        "kind": "prompt",
                        "config": {"output_contract": prompt_c},
                    },
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "foreach_images",
                        "kind": "foreach_image",
                        "config": {
                            "item_executor": {"output_contract": item_contract},
                        },
                    },
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "aggregate_images",
                        "kind": "aggregate_case",
                        "config": {"foreach_node_id": "foreach_images"},
                        "output_schema": aggregate_schema,
                    },
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "level_matcher",
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "config": {
                            "module_type_key": "score_level_matcher",
                            "rule_module_version_id": 19,
                            "component_snapshot": {
                                "content_json": json.dumps(
                                    {
                                        "levels": [
                                            {"level": "L1", "min_score": 80},
                                            {"level": "L2", "min_score": 0},
                                        ]
                                    }
                                )
                            },
                        },
                        "output_schema": {
                            "type": "object",
                            "properties": {
                                "level": {"type": "string", "title": "等级"}
                            },
                        },
                    },
                ],
                "edges": [
                    {"from": "prompt_a", "to": "prompt_b"},
                    {"from": "prompt_b", "to": "prompt_c"},
                    {"from": "prompt_c", "to": "foreach_images"},
                    {"from": "foreach_images", "to": "aggregate_images"},
                    {"from": "aggregate_images", "to": "level_matcher"},
                ],
            },
        }
    }

    frozen = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(snapshot)
    by_key = {node["node_key"]: node for node in frozen["nodes"]}

    assert {
        "workflow.prompt_a.scope",
        "workflow.prompt_b.aesthetic_score",
        "workflow.prompt_c.tags",
        "workflow.foreach_images.items[*].result.score",
        "workflow.foreach_images.items[*].result.details.style",
        "workflow.aggregate_images.score.mean",
    }.issubset(by_key)
    assert not any(
        key.startswith(("production.", "judgement.", "v3."))
        for key in by_key
    )
    foreach_field = by_key[
        "workflow.foreach_images.items[*].result.details.style"
    ]
    expected_hash = hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(
            item_contract,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    assert foreach_field["metadata"]["output_contract_hash"] == expected_hash
    assert foreach_field["metadata"]["field_identity"] == {
        "node_instance_id": "foreach_images",
        "output_contract_hash": expected_hash,
        "field_path": "details.style",
        "item_key": "*",
    }
    aggregate_field = by_key["workflow.aggregate_images.score.mean"]
    assert aggregate_field["metadata"]["editable"] is False
    assert aggregate_field["metadata"]["read_only_reason"] == (
        "案例聚合身份、计数、哈希和派生统计由运行时重算，只能查看"
    )
    assert aggregate_field["metadata"]["field_identity"]["field_path"] == (
        "score.mean"
    )
    assert by_key["workflow.prompt_a.scope"]["metadata"][
        "paid_rerun_required"
    ] is True
    assert by_key["workflow.prompt_b.aesthetic_score"]["metadata"][
        "paid_rerun_required"
    ] is True
    assert by_key["workflow.prompt_c.tags"]["metadata"].get(
        "paid_rerun_required"
    ) is not True
    assert by_key[
        "workflow.foreach_images.items[*].result.score"
    ]["metadata"].get("paid_rerun_required") is not True


def test_same_field_path_keeps_contract_hashes_separate_between_versions() -> None:
    old = _strict_contract(
        "score_v1",
        {"score": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 5}},
    )
    new = _strict_contract(
        "score_v2",
        {"score": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 100}},
    )

    def frozen(contract: dict) -> dict:
        return freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(
            {
                "workflow": {
                    "version": "1",
                    "manifest": {
                        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                        "category_key": "inspiration_image",
                        "nodes": [
                            {
                                "node_instance_id": "unified",
                                "kind": "prompt",
                                "config": {"output_contract": contract},
                            }
                        ],
                        "edges": [],
                    },
                }
            }
        )["nodes"][0]

    old_field = frozen(old)
    new_field = frozen(new)

    assert old_field["metadata"]["field_identity"]["field_path"] == "score"
    assert new_field["metadata"]["field_identity"]["field_path"] == "score"
    assert old_field["metadata"]["output_contract_hash"] != new_field["metadata"][
        "output_contract_hash"
    ]
    assert old_field["compatibility_key"] != new_field["compatibility_key"]


def test_frozen_workflow_fields_follow_node_and_schema_property_order() -> None:
    contract = _strict_contract(
        "ordered",
        {
            "z_first": {"type": "string", "title": "先展示"},
            "a_second": {"type": "string", "title": "后展示"},
        },
    )
    frozen = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(
        {
            "workflow": {
                "version": "1",
                "manifest": {
                    "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                    "category_key": "inspiration_image",
                    "nodes": [
                        {
                            "node_instance_id": "prompt_z",
                            "kind": "prompt",
                            "config": {"output_contract": contract},
                        },
                        {
                            "node_instance_id": "prompt_a",
                            "kind": "prompt",
                            "config": {"output_contract": contract},
                        },
                    ],
                    "edges": [{"from": "prompt_z", "to": "prompt_a"}],
                },
            }
        }
    )

    assert [node["node_key"] for node in frozen["nodes"]] == [
        "workflow.prompt_z.z_first",
        "workflow.prompt_z.a_second",
        "workflow.prompt_a.z_first",
        "workflow.prompt_a.a_second",
    ]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_correction_contract_legacy.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

from app.correction_view import build_correction_view, legacy_correction_view_from_archived_fields


def _item(archive: dict) -> SimpleNamespace:
    run = SimpleNamespace(
        id=41,
        category_key="inspiration_image",
        __tablename__="baseline_regression_runs",
        correction_contract_json=None,
        correction_contract_hash=None,
        execution_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {"active_contract_that_must_not_be_read": {"nodes": [{"node_key": "must-not-read"}]}},
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
    )
    evaluation = SimpleNamespace(
        id=51,
        review_revision=2,
        correction_history_json="[]",
        precheck_json=json.dumps({"production_fields": {"title": "归档标题"}}, ensure_ascii=False),
        aesthetic_json="{}",
        scoring_json=json.dumps({"score": 70, "level": "L2"}, ensure_ascii=False),
        level="L2",
        score=70,
        reviews=[],
    )
    snapshot = {
        "archived_correction_fields": archive,
        "stage_a": {"production_fields": {"title": "归档标题"}},
        "predicted_level": "L2",
        "authoritative_score": 70,
    }
    return SimpleNamespace(
        id=61,
        run_id=41,
        run=run,
        evaluation_id=51,
        evaluation=evaluation,
        result_snapshot_json=json.dumps(snapshot, ensure_ascii=False),
    )


def _complete_node() -> dict:
    return {
        "node_key": "call_a.title",
        "layer": "A",
        "path": "call_a.title",
        "label": "素材标题",
        "description": "历史归档的素材标题纠偏字段",
        "type": "text",
        "semantic_version": "1",
        "compatibility_key": "production-field:title",
        "required": True,
        "evidence": {"description": "请提供图片证据", "required": False},
        "value": "归档标题",
    }


def test_complete_archived_subset_is_editable_without_active_fallback() -> None:
    view = legacy_correction_view_from_archived_fields(
        _item({"expected_nodes": ["call_a.title"], "nodes": [_complete_node()]})
    )

    assert view["snapshot_status"] == "legacy_compatible"
    assert view["read_only"] is False
    assert view["snapshot_source"] == "archived_item_fields"
    assert [node["node_key"] for node in view["nodes"]] == ["call_a.title"]
    assert view["nodes"][0]["editable"] is True
    assert view["nodes"][0]["model_value"] == "归档标题"
    assert view["nodes"][0].get("human_value") is None


def test_incomplete_archive_is_explicitly_read_only_and_lists_unavailable_nodes() -> None:
    incomplete = _complete_node()
    incomplete.pop("description")
    view = legacy_correction_view_from_archived_fields(
        _item(
            {
                "expected_nodes": ["call_a.title", "v3.final_level"],
                "nodes": [incomplete],
            }
        )
    )

    assert view["snapshot_status"] == "legacy_read_only"
    assert view["read_only"] is True
    assert "v3.final_level" in view["unavailable_nodes"]
    assert any("description" in item for item in view["unavailable_nodes"])
    assert all(node["editable"] is False for node in view["nodes"])


def test_build_view_uses_item_archive_when_run_has_no_contract() -> None:
    item = _item({"expected_nodes": ["call_a.title"], "nodes": [_complete_node()]})
    view = build_correction_view(None, run=item.run, item=item)

    assert view["snapshot_source"] == "archived_item_fields"
    assert view["contract"]["contract_hash"]
    assert [node["node_key"] for node in view["nodes"]] == ["call_a.title"]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_correction_contract_migration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect

from app.correction_contract import (
    assert_correction_contract_complete,
    correction_contract_from_run_snapshot,
    freeze_contract_from_execution_snapshot,
    freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot,
    freeze_correction_contract,
)
from app.evaluation_component_catalog import MODULE_TYPE_KEYS
from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS
from app.inspiration_category_seed import (
    build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions,
    build_inspiration_v3_contract,
)


def test_migration_75_adds_nullable_contract_columns_idempotently(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'correction-contract-v75.db'}")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE baseline_regression_runs (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, execution_snapshot_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}')"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE evaluation_production_runs (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, category_profile_snapshot_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}')"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE prompt_regression_runs (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, baseline_strategy_snapshot_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', candidate_strategy_snapshot_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}')"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO baseline_regression_runs (id, execution_snapshot_json) VALUES (1, '{\"legacy\":true}')"
            )
            migration = next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 75)
            migration.up(connection)
            migration.up(connection)

            for table in (
                "baseline_regression_runs",
                "evaluation_production_runs",
                "prompt_regression_runs",
            ):
                columns = {column["name"] for column in inspect(connection).get_columns(table)}
                assert {"correction_contract_json", "correction_contract_hash"} <= columns
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT execution_snapshot_json FROM baseline_regression_runs WHERE id=1"
            ).scalar_one() == '{"legacy":true}'
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_freeze_contract_is_canonical_and_snapshot_lookup_is_immutable() -> None:
    contract = freeze_correction_contract(
        category_key="inspiration_image",
        prompt_snapshot={"a": "提示词A"},
        dimension_snapshot={"dimensions": [{"key": "composition"}]},
        production_field_snapshot={"fields": [{"key": "title"}]},
        v3_snapshot={
            "nodes": [
                {
                    "node_key": "v3.final",
                    "layer": "V3",
                    "path": "final_level",
                    "order": 1,
                    "label": "最终等级",
                    "description": "根据冻结规则确定最终等级",
                    "type": "enum",
                    "options": ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"],
                    "semantic_version": "1",
                    "compatibility_key": "final-level",
                    "required": True,
                    "evidence": {"description": "需要等级判断证据"},
                    "recompute_ref": "scoring.final_level",
                }
            ]
        },
    )

    assert contract["contract_hash"]
    assert correction_contract_from_run_snapshot(contract) == contract
    snapshot = json.loads(json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False))
    snapshot["nodes"].append({"node_key": "new"})
    assert correction_contract_from_run_snapshot(contract)["nodes"] != snapshot["nodes"]


def test_workflow_snapshot_derives_ordered_adaptive_correction_nodes() -> None:
    workflow_snapshot = {
        "workflow": {
            "version": "7",
            "canonical_hash": "b" * 64,
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v2",
                "category_key": "model_3d_su",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "key": "input",
                        "kind": "upstream_connector",
                        "display_name": "读取素材",
                        "config": {"connector_key": "fixture.input"},
                    },
                    {
                        "key": "prompt-c",
                        "kind": "prompt",
                        "display_name": "调用提示词 C",
                        "config": {
                            "prompt_ref": "prompt:C@workflow-v1",
                            "model_registry_id": 8,
                            "max_tokens": 4096,
                            "thinking_level": "medium",
                        },
                    },
                    {
                        "key": "grade",
                        "kind": "level_matcher",
                        "display_name": "等级撮合",
                        "config": {
                            "matcher_ref": "matcher:model_3d_su@r3",
                            "rule_revision_id": 13,
                        },
                    },
                    {
                        "key": "output",
                        "kind": "downstream_projection",
                        "display_name": "写入知识图谱",
                        "config": {"connector_key": "fixture.output"},
                    },
                ],
                "edges": [
                    {"from": "input", "to": "prompt-c"},
                    {"from": "prompt-c", "to": "grade"},
                    {"from": "grade", "to": "output"},
                ],
            },
        },
        "prompts": [
            {
                "reference": "prompt:C@workflow-v1",
                "prompt_id": 21,
                "version": "workflow-v1",
                "content_sha256": "c" * 64,
            }
        ],
        "models": [
            {
                "model_registry_id": 8,
                "name": "运营评测模型",
                "model_id": "model-8",
            }
        ],
        "rules": [
            {
                "rule_revision_id": 13,
                "revision": 3,
                "contract_hash": "d" * 64,
            }
        ],
    }

    contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(workflow_snapshot)

    assert_correction_contract_complete(contract)
    assert contract["contract_version"] == "workflow-correction-contract-v1"
    assert contract["workflow"]["version"] == "7"
    by_key = {node["node_key"]: node for node in contract["nodes"]}
    assert by_key["workflow.prompt-c"]["metadata"]["editable"] is True
    assert by_key["workflow.prompt-c"]["metadata"]["prompt_ref"] == (
        "prompt:C@workflow-v1"
    )
    assert by_key["workflow.prompt-c"]["metadata"]["model_registry_id"] == 8
    assert by_key["workflow.grade"]["metadata"]["rule_revision_id"] == 13
    assert by_key["workflow.input"]["metadata"]["editable"] is False
    assert by_key["workflow.output"]["metadata"]["editable"] is False


def _workflow_v3_correction_snapshot() -> dict:
    nodes = [
        {
            "node_instance_id": "input",
            "kind": "upstream_connector",
            "display_name": "读取待评测素材",
            "config": {"connector_key": "fixture.input"},
            "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        },
        {
            "node_instance_id": "prompt_c",
            "kind": "prompt",
            "display_name": "知识图谱字段提取",
            "config": {
                "asset_key": "prompt_c",
                "prompt_version_id": 31,
                "model_registry_id": 7,
                "max_tokens": 8192,
                "thinking_level": "high",
                "component_snapshot": {
                    "kind": "prompt",
                    "version_id": 31,
                    "version": "component-v2",
                    "content_hash": "a" * 64,
                },
                "model_snapshot": {
                    "model_registry_id": 7,
                    "name": "主评测模型",
                    "model_id": "fixture-model",
                },
            },
            "output_schema": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "category_name": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "title": "类目名称",
                        "description": "知识图谱中的类目名称",
                    },
                    "scene_tags": {
                        "type": "array",
                        "title": "场景标签",
                        "description": "知识图谱场景标签列表",
                    },
                },
            },
        },
    ]
    previous = "prompt_c"
    for index, module_type in enumerate(MODULE_TYPE_KEYS, start=1):
        node_id = f"{module_type}_{index}"
        nodes.append(
            {
                "node_instance_id": node_id,
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "display_name": f"规则模块 {index}",
                "config": {
                    "asset_key": module_type,
                    "module_type_key": module_type,
                    "rule_module_version_id": 40 + index,
                    "input_bindings": {"previous": f"{previous}.output"},
                    "scope": "stock",
                    "component_snapshot": {
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "version_id": 40 + index,
                        "version": str(index),
                        "module_type_key": module_type,
                        "content_hash": str(index) * 64,
                    },
                },
                "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
            }
        )
        previous = node_id
    nodes.append(
        {
            "node_instance_id": "eligibility_redline_repeat",
            "kind": "rule_module",
            "display_name": "再次检查准入与红线",
            "config": {
                "asset_key": "eligibility_redline",
                "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
                "rule_module_version_id": 99,
                "input_bindings": {"previous": f"{previous}.output"},
                "scope": "stock",
                "component_snapshot": {
                    "kind": "rule_module",
                    "version_id": 99,
                    "version": "2",
                    "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
                    "content_hash": "f" * 64,
                },
            },
            "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        }
    )
    nodes.append(
        {
            "node_instance_id": "output",
            "kind": "downstream_projection",
            "display_name": "写入评测结果",
            "config": {"connector_key": "fixture.output"},
            "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        }
    )
    identities = [node["node_instance_id"] for node in nodes]
    return {
        "workflow": {
            "workflow_definition_id": 10,
            "workflow_version_id": 20,
            "version": "5",
            "canonical_hash": "e" * 64,
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "category_key": "space_image",
                "nodes": nodes,
                "edges": [
                    {"from": source, "to": target}
                    for source, target in zip(identities, identities[1:])
                ],
            },
        }
    }


def test_workflow_v3_correction_contract_keeps_repeated_module_instances() -> None:
    contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(
        _workflow_v3_correction_snapshot()
    )

    assert_correction_contract_complete(contract)
    instance_ids = {
        node["metadata"]["node_instance_id"] for node in contract["nodes"]
    }
    assert {
        "eligibility_redline_1",
        "eligibility_redline_repeat",
    } <= instance_ids
    repeat_nodes = [
        node
        for node in contract["nodes"]
        if node["metadata"]["node_instance_id"]
        == "eligibility_redline_repeat"
    ]
    assert repeat_nodes
    assert all(
        node["metadata"]["component_version"]["version_id"] == 99
        for node in repeat_nodes
    )
    assert all(node["metadata"]["editable"] is True for node in repeat_nodes)


def test_each_rule_module_and_prompt_schema_have_business_correction_fields() -> None:
    contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(
        _workflow_v3_correction_snapshot()
    )

    fields_by_module = {
        module_type: {
            node["metadata"]["module_field_key"]
            for node in contract["nodes"]
            if node["metadata"].get("module_type_key") == module_type
        }
        for module_type in MODULE_TYPE_KEYS
    }
    assert fields_by_module == {
        "eligibility_redline": {"hit", "matched_rules", "level_cap", "reason"},
        "track_routing": {"track_key", "confidence", "reason"},
        "media_adjustment": {"media_type", "score_adjustment", "reason"},
        "quality_cap_exception": {
            "quality_grade",
            "score_cap",
            "exception_applied",
            "reason",
        },
        "dimension_scoring": {"dimensions", "total_score", "reason"},
        "anchor_calibration": {"anchor_id", "calibrated_score", "reason"},
        "score_level_matcher": {"score", "level", "reason"},
    }
    prompt_nodes = [
        node
        for node in contract["nodes"]
        if node["metadata"].get("node_instance_id") == "prompt_c"
    ]
    assert {node["metadata"]["output_field_key"] for node in prompt_nodes} == {
        "category_name",
        "scene_tags",
    }
    assert all(
        node["metadata"]["component_version"]["version"] == "component-v2"
        for node in prompt_nodes
    )
    assert all(
        node["metadata"]["model_version"]["name"] == "主评测模型"
        for node in prompt_nodes
    )
    assert all(
        node["metadata"]["selected_parameters"]
        == {
                "prompt_version_id": 31,
                "model_registry_id": 7,
                "max_tokens": 8192,
                "thinking_level": "none",
                "thinking_enabled": False,
            }
        for node in prompt_nodes
    )

                        "key": "title",

                        "description": "用于检索与推荐的素材标题",
                        "type": "text",
                        "required": True,
                    },
                    {
                        "key": "tags",
                        "label": "素材标签",
                        "description": "用于检索与推荐的素材标签",
                        "type": "list",
                        "required": True,

def test_execution_snapshot_uses_compatibility_fields_and_v3_rule_nodes() -> None:
    v3_contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
    snapshot = {
        "pipeline_config": {},
        "dimension_contract": {"definition": {"dimensions": []}},
        "v3_authoritative_bundle": {
            "contract": v3_contract,
            "classification_map": {},
            "subcategory_dimensions": build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions(),
        },
    }

    contract = freeze_contract_from_execution_snapshot(
        category_key="inspiration_image",
        execution_snapshot=snapshot,
    )

    assert_correction_contract_complete(contract)
    by_key = {node["node_key"]: node for node in contract["nodes"]}
    assert {
        "call_a.title",
        "call_a.tags",
        "call_a.reason",
        "call_a.trait",
        "v3.track_key",
        "v3.level_thresholds",
        "v3.final_level",
    } <= set(by_key)
    assert any(node["layer"] == "B" for node in contract["nodes"])
    b_nodes = [node for node in contract["nodes"] if node["layer"] == "B"]
    assert all(node["node_key"].startswith("call_b.") for node in b_nodes)
    # 调用B先给出整体美感基础分，再逐条判断规则命中，所以 B 层同时包含这两类节点。
    rule_nodes = [
        node for node in b_nodes if node["node_key"] != "call_b.aesthetic_score"
    ]
    assert rule_nodes, "冻结的维度规则必须展开成逐条可纠偏的 B 层节点"
    assert all(node["type"] == "rule_hit" for node in rule_nodes)
    aesthetic = by_key["call_b.aesthetic_score"]
    assert aesthetic["type"] == "integer", "美感基础分是 0-100 整数，不是规则命中"

def _graded_workflow_snapshot() -> dict:
    """等级评测形态的工作流快照：规则内容按真实结构冻结在 content_json 里。"""

    def module(node_key: str, module_type_key: str, content: dict) -> dict:
        return {
            "key": node_key,
            "kind": "rule_module",
            "display_name": module_type_key,
            "config": {
                "module_type_key": module_type_key,
                "component_snapshot": {
                    "kind": "rule_module",
                    "module_type_key": module_type_key,
                    "content_json": json.dumps(content, ensure_ascii=False),
                },
            },
        }

    return {
        "workflow": {
            "workflow_definition_id": 4,
            "workflow_version_id": 107,
            "version": "11",
            "canonical_hash": "e" * 64,
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "category_key": "inspiration_image",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "key": "prompt_a",
                        "kind": "prompt",
                        "display_name": "调用A",
                        "config": {"prompt_ref": "prompt:A@v6"},
                    },
                    {
                        "key": "prompt_b",
                        "kind": "prompt",
                        "display_name": "调用B",
                        "config": {"prompt_ref": "prompt:B@v30"},
                    },
                    module(
                        "track_routing_2",
                        "track_routing",
                        {
                            "tracks": [
                                {"key": "class_one", "label": "一类设计素材"},
                                {"key": "class_two", "label": "二类设计素材"},
                                {"key": "class_three", "label": "三类非设计素材"},
                            ],
                            "default_track": "class_three",
                        },
                    ),
                    module(
                        "quality_cap_exception_4",
                        "quality_cap_exception",
                        {
                            "high_score_veto": {
                                "enabled": True,
                                "rules": [
                                    {
                                        "key": "blurry_grayish",
                                        "description": "模糊发灰",
                                        "severity": "A",
                                        "source": "hard_defects",
                                    },
                                    {
                                        "key": "large_area_watermark",
                                        "description": "大面积水印",
                                        "severity": "watermark_heavy",
                                        "source": "image_defects",
                                    },
                                ],
                            }
                        },
                    ),
                    module(
                        "score_level_matcher_7",
                        "score_level_matcher",
                        {
                            "levels": [
                                {"level": "L1", "min_score": 90, "enabled": True},
                                {"level": "L2", "min_score": 80, "enabled": True},
                                {"level": "L3", "min_score": 60, "enabled": True},
                                {"level": "L4", "enabled": False},
                                {"level": "L5", "min_score": 0, "enabled": True},
                            ]
                        },
                    ),
                ],
                "edges": [
                    {"from": "prompt_a", "to": "track_routing_2"},
                    {"from": "track_routing_2", "to": "prompt_b"},
                    {"from": "prompt_b", "to": "quality_cap_exception_4"},
                    {"from": "quality_cap_exception_4", "to": "score_level_matcher_7"},
                ],
            },
        },
    }


def test_graded_workflow_contract_carries_field_level_correction_nodes() -> None:
    """等级评测工作流的合同必须带上复核页按字段纠偏所需的节点。"""

    contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(_graded_workflow_snapshot())
    assert_correction_contract_complete(contract)

    by_key = {node["node_key"]: node for node in contract["nodes"]}
    # 复核页三段各自查找的键，缺一段那一段就整片显示「未纳入本轮纠偏合同」
    for node_key, node_type in (
        ("call_a.title", "call_a_field"),
        ("call_a.tags", "call_a_field"),
        ("call_a.hard_defects", "precheck_field"),
        ("call_b.aesthetic_score", "aesthetic_score"),
        ("v3.track_key", "track"),
        ("v3.final_level", "final_level"),
    ):
        assert node_key in by_key, f"合同缺少 {node_key}"
        assert by_key[node_key]["metadata"]["node_type"] == node_type

    # 选项跟随冻结规则，不是前端常量
    assert by_key["v3.track_key"]["options"] == [
        "class_one",
        "class_two",
        "class_three",
    ]
    # L4 在这版机制里是关掉的，不能出现在可选等级里
    assert by_key["v3.final_level"]["options"] == ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L5"]
    assert "blurry_grayish" in by_key["call_a.hard_defects"]["options"]

    # 取值路径要落在复核页实际读得到的位置上
    assert by_key["call_a.hard_defects"]["path"] == "precheck.hard_defects"
    assert by_key["call_b.aesthetic_score"]["path"] == "aesthetic.aesthetic_score"
    assert by_key["v3.final_level"]["path"] == "scoring.level"

    # 原有的流程节点不能被挤掉
    assert "workflow.prompt_a.output" in by_key


def test_field_extraction_workflow_contract_stays_node_only() -> None:
    """没有等级刻度的工作流（例如按字段抽取的类目）不应被塞进等级相关节点。"""

    snapshot = _graded_workflow_snapshot()
    nodes = snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
    snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"] = [
        node
        for node in nodes
        if (node.get("config") or {}).get("module_type_key") != "score_level_matcher"
    ]
    snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["category_key"] = "knowledge_graph"

    contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(snapshot)
    node_keys = {node["node_key"] for node in contract["nodes"]}
    assert not any(key.startswith(("call_a.", "call_b.", "v3.")) for key in node_keys)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_correction_deductions.py =====
from copy import deepcopy
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app.correction_contract import freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot
from app.correction_deductions import annotate_deductions
from app.correction_view import build_correction_view, submit_correction_nodes, CorrectionViewError


def fixture():
    rules = [{"rule_id": "minor_defect", "deduction": 20, "description": "局部细节稍弱"},
             {"rule_id": "obvious_defect", "deduction": 50, "description": "关键装配明显简化"}]
    hit_schema = {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "object", "properties": {
        "rule_id": {"type": "string", "enum": [rule["rule_id"] for rule in rules]},
        "evidence": {"type": "string"}, "confidence": {"type": "string"}}}}
    manifest = {"schema_version": "workflow-v3", "category_key": "model_3d_su", "edges": [], "nodes": [
        {"node_instance_id": "prompt_b", "kind": "prompt", "config": {"asset_key": "prompt_b"},
         "output_schema": {"type": "object", "properties": {"dimensions": {"type": "object", "properties": {
             "model_detail": {"type": "object", "properties": {"hit_rules": hit_schema}}}}}}},
        {"node_instance_id": "dimensions", "kind": "rule_module", "config": {
            "module_type_key": "dimension_scoring", "component_snapshot": {"content_json": json.dumps({
                "subcategory_dimensions": {"space_building": {"common_group": {"schema_definition": {"dimensions": [
                    {"key": "model_detail", "label": "模型细节", "deduction_rules": rules}]}}},
                    "soft_furnishing": {"common_group": {"schema_definition": {"dimensions": [
                        {"key": "model_detail", "deduction_rules": [{"rule_id": "wrong_track", "deduction": 99}]}]}}}}})}},
         "output_schema": {"type": "object"}},
        {"node_instance_id": "anchor", "kind": "rule_module", "config": {"module_type_key": "anchor_calibration"},
         "output_schema": {"type": "object"}},
    ]}
    def strict_schema(schema):
        if schema.get("type") == "object":
            schema["additionalProperties"] = False
            schema["required"] = list(schema["properties"])
            for child in schema["properties"].values():
                strict_schema(child)
        if schema.get("type") == "array":
            strict_schema(schema["items"])
    strict_schema(manifest["nodes"][0]["output_schema"])
    manifest["nodes"][0]["config"]["output_contract"] = {"mode": "json_schema", "name": "deduction_test", "strict": True, "schema": manifest["nodes"][0]["output_schema"]}
    snapshot = {"workflow": {"workflow_definition_id": 1, "workflow_version_id": 2, "version": "2", "canonical_hash": "a" * 64, "manifest": manifest}}
    contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(snapshot)
    detail = {"hit_rules": [{"rule_id": "obvious_defect", "evidence": "接缝缺失", "confidence": "high"}],
              "share": 20, "raw_rule_deduction": 50, "applied_rule_deduction": 50, "point_deduction": 10}
    scoring = {"score": 47, "level": "L3", "track_key": "space_building", "dimension_evidence": {
        "mode": "rule_deduction", "initial_score": 77, "applied_deduction_total": 30.5,
        "dimension_evidence": {"model_detail": detail}}, "steps": []}
    evaluation = SimpleNamespace(review_revision=0, correction_history_json="[]", scoring_json=json.dumps(scoring),
                                 aesthetic_json="{}", precheck_json="{}", workflow_node_outputs_json="{}", score=47, level="L3", reviews=[])
    run = SimpleNamespace(id=429, category_key="model_3d_su", correction_contract_json=json.dumps(contract),
                          correction_contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"], execution_snapshot_json=json.dumps({"workflow_snapshot": snapshot}))
    item = SimpleNamespace(id=87723, run_id=429, asset_id=1, evaluation_id=1, evaluation=evaluation, result_snapshot_json="{}")
    return run, item, contract, scoring, snapshot


def test_breakdown_uses_frozen_track_and_actual_weighted_points_without_mutating_contract():
    run, item, contract, _, _ = fixture()
    before = deepcopy(contract)
    view = build_correction_view(None, run=run, item=item)
    nodes = {n["node_key"]: n for n in view["nodes"]}
    derived = nodes["workflow.dimensions.dimensions"]
    row = derived["metadata"]["deduction_breakdown"]["rows"][0]
    assert row["point_deduction"] == 10
    assert row["hits"][0]["deduction"] == 50
    assert row["hits"][0]["description"] == "关键装配明显简化"
    target = nodes[row["correction_node_key"]]
    assert target["metadata"]["output_field_key"] == "dimensions.model_detail.hit_rules"
    assert [r["rule_id"] for r in target["metadata"]["deduction_rule_options"]] == ["minor_defect", "obvious_defect"]
    assert derived["editable"] is False
    assert nodes["workflow.anchor.calibrated_score"]["label"] == "美感初始分（扣分前）"
    assert nodes["workflow.anchor.calibrated_score"]["current_value"] == 77
    assert contract == before
    assert view["contract"]["contract_hash"] == contract["contract_hash"]


def test_derived_score_write_is_rejected_before_any_database_mutation():
    run, item, contract, _, _ = fixture()
    with pytest.raises(CorrectionViewError) as caught:
        submit_correction_nodes(None, run=run, item=item, contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"],
            nodes=[{"node_key": "workflow.dimensions.total_score", "human_value": 99, "reason": "不能直接覆盖", "evidence": []}],
            review_revision=0, idempotency_key="derived-score", actor="tester")
    assert caught.value.code == "CORRECTION_DERIVED_SCORE_READ_ONLY"


def test_missing_snapshot_never_borrows_an_active_rule_and_other_scoring_modes_unchanged():
    _, _, contract, scoring, _ = fixture()
    nodes = deepcopy(contract["nodes"])
    annotate_deductions(nodes, scoring, {})
    derived = next(n for n in nodes if n["node_key"] == "workflow.dimensions.dimensions")
    row = derived["metadata"]["deduction_breakdown"]["rows"][0]
    assert row["hits"][0]["description"] is None
    assert row["point_deduction"] == 10
    nodes = deepcopy(contract["nodes"])
    original = deepcopy(nodes)
    annotate_deductions(nodes, {"dimension_evidence": {"mode": "bonus_cap_v2"}}, {})
    assert nodes == original

===== FILE backend/tests/test_correction_display_label_fallback.py =====
"""纠偏契约的展示层 label 必须永远含中文，否则整页退化成只读。

validate_correction_contract 要求每个节点的 label 和 description 含中文。工作流
冻结的严格输出 schema 只给字段名和类型，没有 title，于是历史契约里全是裸英文
label——2026-09-14 林周金实测 run333（灵感图，72 节点）46 项报「必须是非空中文
描述」，纠偏页整页只读，一个字段都改不了。

映射表永远会落后于新字段，所以这里既锁精确中文名，也锁兜底：漏一个字段不能再
让运营完全无法纠偏。
"""

from app.correction_contract import (
    correction_contract_hash,
    correction_display_contract,
    validate_correction_contract,
)


def _contract(nodes):
    return {
        "contract_version": "v1",
        "category_key": "inspiration_image",
        "nodes": nodes,
    }


def _node(node_key, label, **extra):
    node = {
        "node_key": node_key,
        "layer": "A",
        "path": node_key,
        "label": label,
        "description": "",
        "type": "text",
        "semantic_version": "1",
        "compatibility_key": node_key,
        "required": False,
        "evidence": {"description": "请填写人工证据", "required": False},
    }
    node.update(extra)
    return node


def test_inspiration_bare_english_labels_no_longer_force_read_only():
    """run333 的真实症状：整份契约因裸英文 label 判失败 → 纠偏页只读。"""

    contract = _contract([
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.classification_confidence", "classification_confidence"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.decision_status", "decision_status"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.uncertain_fields", "uncertain_fields"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_b.dimensions.color_aesthetics.grade", "grade"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_b.source_deductions", "source_deductions"),
    ])
    assert len(validate_correction_contract(contract)) > 0
    assert validate_correction_contract(contract, presentation_overlay=True) == []

    shown = {n["node_key"]: n["label"] for n in correction_display_contract(contract)["nodes"]}
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.classification_confidence"] == "分类置信度"
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.decision_status"] == "判定状态"
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_b.dimensions.color_aesthetics.grade"] == "色彩美学 · 评级"
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_b.source_deductions"] == "来源扣分"


def test_redline_children_reuse_existing_business_wording():
    """红线子项直接复用既有业务口径，不另起一套说法。"""

    contract = _contract([
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.redline_triggered.screenshot", "screenshot"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.redline_triggered.large_area_watermark", "large_area_watermark"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.redline_triggered.upside_down_or_mirrored", "upside_down_or_mirrored"),
    ])
    shown = {n["node_key"]: n["label"] for n in correction_display_contract(contract)["nodes"]}
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.redline_triggered.screenshot"] == "红线命中：是截图"
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.redline_triggered.large_area_watermark"] == "红线命中：有大面积水印"
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.redline_triggered.upside_down_or_mirrored"] == "红线命中：是颠倒图"
    assert validate_correction_contract(contract, presentation_overlay=True) == []


def test_unknown_field_falls_back_instead_of_blanking_the_page():
    """映射表永远落后于新字段——漏一个也不能让整页只读。

    这是下限保证：兜底保留原字段名，运营仍能对上是哪一项。
    """

    contract = _contract([
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.some_brand_new_field", "some_brand_new_field"),
    ])
    assert len(validate_correction_contract(contract)) > 0
    assert validate_correction_contract(contract, presentation_overlay=True) == []
    node = correction_display_contract(contract)["nodes"][0]
    assert node["label"] == "提示词输出字段 some_brand_new_field"
    assert "some_brand_new_field" in node["label"]


def test_existing_chinese_labels_are_left_alone():
    """已中文化的 label 和作者写的 title 不被兜底覆盖。"""

    contract = _contract([
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.title", "运营自定义标题口径"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.decision_status", "本版专用判定状态"),
    ])
    shown = {n["node_key"]: n["label"] for n in correction_display_contract(contract)["nodes"]}
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.title"] == "运营自定义标题口径"
    assert shown["workflow.prompt_a.decision_status"] == "本版专用判定状态"


def test_display_overlay_never_touches_identity_or_field_set():
    """展示层只改 label/description：字段集合、类型和存库 hash 都不能变。"""

    contract = _contract([
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.decision_status", "decision_status"),
        _node("workflow.prompt_a.model_3d_su_fields.watermark", "watermark", type="enum"),
    ])
    before_hash = correction_contract_hash(contract)
    shown = correction_display_contract(contract)
    assert correction_contract_hash(contract) == before_hash  # 原对象未被就地改动
    assert [n["node_key"] for n in shown["nodes"]] == [n["node_key"] for n in contract["nodes"]]
    assert [n["type"] for n in shown["nodes"]] == [n["type"] for n in contract["nodes"]]

        _node("workflow.prompt_a.model_3d_su_fields.qr_code", "qr_code", type="enum"),
