        normalize_design_case_dimension_expected_result(
            {"level": "L2", "images": [{"cloud_id": "1", "level": "L2"}]},
            workflow_input={"case": {"images": []}},
        )
    assert excinfo.value.code == "design_case_dimension_truth_invalid"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_design_case_image_grade.py =====
import pytest

from app.design_case_image_grade import (
    DEFAULT_IMAGE_GRADE_BANDS,
    DesignCaseImageGradeError,
    build_image_grade_projection,
    compare_image_grades,
    grade_for_score,
    normalize_grade_bands,
)


def _aggregate(results):
    return {"schema_version": "workflow-case-aggregate-v1", "results": results}


def test_default_bands_follow_operator_mapping():
    """运营 2026-09-09 口径：优 85-100、中 72-84、中差 60-71、极差 0-59。"""

    assert grade_for_score(100, DEFAULT_IMAGE_GRADE_BANDS) == "L1"
    assert grade_for_score(85, DEFAULT_IMAGE_GRADE_BANDS) == "L1"
    assert grade_for_score(84, DEFAULT_IMAGE_GRADE_BANDS) == "L2"
    assert grade_for_score(72, DEFAULT_IMAGE_GRADE_BANDS) == "L2"
    assert grade_for_score(71, DEFAULT_IMAGE_GRADE_BANDS) == "L3"
    assert grade_for_score(60, DEFAULT_IMAGE_GRADE_BANDS) == "L3"
    assert grade_for_score(59, DEFAULT_IMAGE_GRADE_BANDS) == "L4"
    assert grade_for_score(0, DEFAULT_IMAGE_GRADE_BANDS) == "L4"


def test_bands_are_configurable_without_code_change():
    bands = normalize_grade_bands(
        [
            {"level": "L1", "min_inclusive": 92},
            {"level": "L2", "min_inclusive": 81},
            {"level": "L3", "min_inclusive": 67},
            {"level": "L4", "min_inclusive": 0},
        ]
    )
    assert grade_for_score(88, bands) == "L2"
    assert grade_for_score(92, bands) == "L1"


def test_bands_must_bottom_out_at_zero():
    """图片维度没有过滤档，最低一档必须兜住 0 分，否则回归会在半路抛错。"""

    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseImageGradeError) as excinfo:
        normalize_grade_bands(
            [
                {"level": "L1", "min_inclusive": 85},
                {"level": "L2", "min_inclusive": 72},
                {"level": "L3", "min_inclusive": 60},
                {"level": "L4", "min_inclusive": 40},
            ]
        )
    assert excinfo.value.code == "image_grade_bands_invalid"


def test_bands_reject_l5():
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseImageGradeError):
        normalize_grade_bands([{"level": "L5", "min_inclusive": 0}])


def test_bands_reject_incomplete_or_unordered():
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseImageGradeError):
        normalize_grade_bands(
            [{"level": "L1", "min_inclusive": 85}, {"level": "L4", "min_inclusive": 0}]
        )
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseImageGradeError):
        normalize_grade_bands(
            [
                {"level": "L1", "min_inclusive": 60},
                {"level": "L2", "min_inclusive": 72},
                {"level": "L3", "min_inclusive": 85},
                {"level": "L4", "min_inclusive": 0},
            ]
        )


def test_projection_uses_cloud_id_and_freezes_bands():
    projection = build_image_grade_projection(
        _aggregate(
            [
                {"item_key": "15775794", "status": "succeeded", "score": 88},
                {"item_key": "15775793", "status": "succeeded", "score": 75},
                {"item_key": "15775795", "status": "succeeded", "score": 40},
            ]
        )
    )
    assert [image["cloud_id"] for image in projection["images"]] == [
        "15775793",
        "15775794",
        "15775795",
    ]
    assert {image["cloud_id"]: image["level"] for image in projection["images"]} == {
        "15775793": "L2",
        "15775794": "L1",
        "15775795": "L4",
    }
    assert projection["level_distribution"] == {"L1": 1, "L2": 1, "L3": 0, "L4": 1}
    # 阈值随结果冻结，事后复盘不用猜当时用的是哪套刻度。
    assert projection["bands"][0] == {"level": "L1", "min_inclusive": 85.0}


def test_projection_does_not_invent_grades_for_unscored_images():
    """非表现图没有分数，失败子项也没有；不猜档位，单独落在 ungraded。"""

    projection = build_image_grade_projection(
        _aggregate(
            [
                {"item_key": "a", "status": "succeeded", "score": 90},
                {"item_key": "b", "status": "succeeded"},
                {"item_key": "c", "status": "failed", "error": {"code": "x"}},
            ]
        )
    )
    assert [image["cloud_id"] for image in projection["images"]] == ["a"]
    assert projection["ungraded"] == [
        {"cloud_id": "b", "reason": "no_score"},
        {"cloud_id": "c", "reason": "failed"},
    ]
    assert projection["graded_images"] == 1


def test_projection_rejects_duplicate_identity():
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseImageGradeError) as excinfo:
        build_image_grade_projection(
            _aggregate(
                [
                    {"item_key": "a", "status": "succeeded", "score": 90},
                    {"item_key": "a", "status": "succeeded", "score": 80},
                ]
            )
        )
    assert excinfo.value.code == "image_grade_item_key_duplicate"


def test_comparison_separates_over_and_under_grading():
    """交付样本实测：误差全是「预测高一档」，方向必须能分开统计。"""

    comparison = compare_image_grades(
        [
            {"cloud_id": "a", "level": "L2"},
            {"cloud_id": "b", "level": "L2"},
            {"cloud_id": "c", "level": "L3"},
        ],
        [
            {"cloud_id": "a", "level": "L1"},
            {"cloud_id": "b", "level": "L2"},
            {"cloud_id": "c", "level": "L4"},
        ],
    )
    assert comparison["compared"] == 3
    assert comparison["correct"] == 1
    assert comparison["over_graded"] == 1
    assert comparison["under_graded"] == 1
    assert comparison["confusion"]["L2"]["L1"] == 1
    assert comparison["confusion"]["L3"]["L4"] == 1
    assert comparison["mismatches"][0] == {
        "cloud_id": "a",
        "expected": "L2",
        "actual": "L1",
    }


def test_comparison_reports_missing_and_unexpected_separately():
    """模型没答（非表现图）和真值没覆盖是两类问题，不能混进准确率。"""

    comparison = compare_image_grades(
        [{"cloud_id": "a", "level": "L2"}, {"cloud_id": "b", "level": "L3"}],
        [{"cloud_id": "a", "level": "L2"}, {"cloud_id": "z", "level": "L1"}],
    )
    assert comparison["compared"] == 1
    assert comparison["accuracy"] == 1.0
    assert comparison["missing"] == ["b"]
    assert comparison["unexpected"] == ["z"]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_design_case_sample_api.py =====
"""素材维度样本上传通道：先预览、确认无误再落库。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Iterator

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.models import Asset, AuditEvent, BaselineSetItem, DesignCaseSample, User
from test_p0e_safe_import import _write_xlsx

CASE_A = (
    "素材标题：谜舍设计 | 长沙罗森尼娜\n"
    "分类：餐饮店铺-蛋糕甜品\n"
    "案例正文前500个字：正文。\n"
    "图片总张图：16张\n"
    "所有表现图的分数：82分有3张,88分有4张\n"
    "所有图片的类型：实景照片有15张,技术图有1张"
)
CASE_B = "素材标题：只有文本的案例\n分类：办公空间\n案例正文前500个字：另一段正文。"
BROKEN = "分类：缺少标题的行\n案例正文前500个字：正文。"


@contextmanager
def _client() -> Iterator[tuple[TestClient, sessionmaker[Session]]]:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False, autoflush=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        user = User(is_admin=True, role="admin", username="case-tester", password_hash="unused", display_name="案例测试员")
        db.add(user)
        db.commit()

    def test_db() -> Iterator[Session]:
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = test_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    try:
        yield TestClient(app), sessions
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        engine.dispose()


def _upload(path: Path):
    return {"file": (path.name, path.read_bytes(),
                     "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet")}


def test_preview_reports_rows_without_writing(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "samples.xlsx"
    _write_xlsx(path, [[CASE_A], [CASE_B], [BROKEN]])
    with _client() as (client, sessions):
        response = client.post("/api/design-case-samples/preview", files=_upload(path))
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        body = response.json()
        assert body["writes_business_database"] is False
        assert body["summary"]["total_rows"] == 3
        assert body["summary"]["usable_rows"] == 2
        assert body["summary"]["rejected_rows"] == 1
        assert body["summary"]["input_kinds"] == {"aggregated": 1, "text_only": 2}
        rejected = next(r for r in body["rows"] if not r["ok"])
        assert rejected["row_number"] == 3
        assert rejected["issues"]
        with sessions() as db:
            assert db.scalars(select(Asset)).all() == []


def test_import_lands_case_assets_and_audits(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "samples.xlsx"
    _write_xlsx(path, [[CASE_A], [CASE_B], [BROKEN]])
    with _client() as (client, sessions):
        response = client.post(
            "/api/design-case-samples/import",
            files=_upload(path),
            data={"package_name": "素材维度样本"},
        )
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        body = response.json()
        assert body["summary"]["created_count"] == 2
        assert body["summary"]["rejected_rows"] == 1
        assert body["rejected"][0]["row_number"] == 3
        assert body["package"]["name"] == "素材维度样本"
        with sessions() as db:
            samples = db.scalars(select(DesignCaseSample)).all()
            assert len(samples) == 2
            assert {s.category_key for s in samples} == {"design_case"}
            assert len(db.scalars(select(Asset)).all()) == 2
            event = db.scalar(
                select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.action == "design_case_sample_imported")
            )
            assert event is not None


def test_reimport_of_same_table_creates_nothing_new(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "samples.xlsx"
    _write_xlsx(path, [[CASE_A], [CASE_B]])
    with _client() as (client, sessions):
        client.post("/api/design-case-samples/import", files=_upload(path))
        again = client.post("/api/design-case-samples/import", files=_upload(path))
        assert again.status_code == 200, again.text
        assert again.json()["summary"] == {
            "created_count": 0, "duplicate_count": 2,
            "aggregated_count": 0, "warned_count": 0, "rejected_rows": 0,
        }
        with sessions() as db:
            assert len(db.scalars(select(Asset)).all()) == 2

        assert ".xlsx" in response.json()["detail"]

def _import(client: TestClient, path: Path) -> dict:
    response = client.post("/api/design-case-samples/import", files=_upload(path))
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    return response.json()


def test_case_package_builds_a_material_dimension_baseline_set(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "samples.xlsx"
    _write_xlsx(path, [[CASE_A], [CASE_B]])
    with _client() as (client, sessions):
        imported = _import(client, path)
        created = client.post(
            "/api/baseline-sets",
            json={
                "name": "素材维度-回归",
                "category_key": "design_case",
                "source_package_id": imported["package"]["id"],
                "package_truth_contract_key": "design_case.material_aggregate.v1",
            },
        )
        assert created.status_code == 200, created.text
        body = created.json()
        # 只有带图片统计的案例进基准集；纯文本案例跳过而不是整包失败
        assert body["item_count"] == 1
        with sessions() as db:
            item = db.scalars(select(BaselineSetItem)).one()
            assert item.truth_contract_key == "design_case.material_aggregate.v1"
            assert item.expected_level is None
            truth = json.loads(item.expected_result_json)
            assert truth["image_total"] == 16
            assert truth["score_distribution"] == {"82": 3, "88": 4}
            assert truth["feature_counts"]["trait"] == {"实景照片": 15, "技术图": 1}


def test_text_only_package_cannot_build_a_material_dimension_set(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "text-only.xlsx"
    _write_xlsx(path, [[CASE_B]])
    with _client() as (client, _):
        imported = _import(client, path)
        created = client.post(
            "/api/baseline-sets",
            json={
                "name": "纯文本",
                "category_key": "design_case",
                "source_package_id": imported["package"]["id"],
                "package_truth_contract_key": "design_case.material_aggregate.v1",
            },
        )
        assert created.status_code == 400
        assert created.json()["detail"]["code"] == "design_case_aggregate_items_empty"


def test_material_dimension_truth_can_be_corrected_after_import(tmp_path) -> None:
    """纠偏循环：运营发现统计有误时能改真值，改动按追加修订留痕。"""

    path = tmp_path / "samples.xlsx"
    _write_xlsx(path, [[CASE_A]])
    with _client() as (client, sessions):
        imported = _import(client, path)
        created = client.post(
            "/api/baseline-sets",
            json={
                "name": "素材维度-纠偏",
                "category_key": "design_case",
                "source_package_id": imported["package"]["id"],
                "package_truth_contract_key": "design_case.material_aggregate.v1",
            },
        )
        assert created.status_code == 200, created.text
        set_id = created.json()["id"]
        with sessions() as db:
            item_id = db.scalars(select(BaselineSetItem)).one().id

        corrected = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-sets/{set_id}/items/{item_id}/truth",
            json={
                "expected_result": {
                    "image_total": 16,
                    "score_distribution": {"82": 3, "88": 5},
                    "feature_counts": {"trait": {"实景照片": 15, "技术图": 1}},
                },
                "truth_contract_key": "design_case.material_aggregate.v1",
                "edit_key": "material-aggregate-fix-1",
                "reason": "运营复核后修正 88 分张数",
            },
        )
        assert corrected.status_code == 200, corrected.text
        assert corrected.json()["expected_result"]["score_distribution"] == {"82": 3, "88": 5}

        # 统计合计不能超过图片总数，写坏的真值当场拒绝
        rejected = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-sets/{set_id}/items/{item_id}/truth",
            json={
                "expected_result": {"image_total": 2, "score_distribution": {"82": 3}},
                "truth_contract_key": "design_case.material_aggregate.v1",
                "edit_key": "material-aggregate-fix-2",
                "reason": "写错的真值",
            },
        )
        assert rejected.status_code == 422
        assert rejected.json()["detail"]["code"] == "design_case_aggregate_truth_invalid"


def test_material_dimension_truth_accepts_the_sample_table_wording(tmp_path) -> None:
    """运营在纠偏页填的是样本表那三行原话，服务端按同一套规则反解成统计。"""

    path = tmp_path / "samples.xlsx"
    _write_xlsx(path, [[CASE_A]])
    with _client() as (client, sessions):
        imported = _import(client, path)
        created = client.post(
            "/api/baseline-sets",
            json={
                "name": "素材维度-文字纠偏",
                "category_key": "design_case",
                "source_package_id": imported["package"]["id"],
                "package_truth_contract_key": "design_case.material_aggregate.v1",
            },
        )
        set_id = created.json()["id"]
        with sessions() as db:
            item_id = db.scalars(select(BaselineSetItem)).one().id

        edited = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-sets/{set_id}/items/{item_id}/truth",
            json={
                "edit_key": "material-text-1",
                "reason": "复核后把 88 分改成 5 张",
                "truth_contract_key": "design_case.material_aggregate.v1",
                "expected_result": {
                    "image_total_text": "16张",
                    "score_distribution_text": "82分有3张,88分有5张",
                    "feature_texts": {"trait": "实景照片有15张,技术图有1张"},
                },
            },
        )
        assert edited.status_code == 200, edited.text
        truth = edited.json()["expected_result"]
        assert truth["image_total"] == 16
        assert truth["score_distribution"] == {"82": 3, "88": 5}
        assert truth["feature_counts"]["trait"] == {"实景照片": 15, "技术图": 1}
        # 文字原话一并留存，下次打开编辑器还是运营自己写的那句
        assert truth["score_distribution_text"] == "82分有3张,88分有5张"


def test_material_dimension_truth_refuses_text_and_counts_that_disagree(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "samples.xlsx"
    _write_xlsx(path, [[CASE_A]])
    with _client() as (client, sessions):
        imported = _import(client, path)
        set_id = client.post(
            "/api/baseline-sets",
            json={
                "name": "素材维度-冲突",
                "category_key": "design_case",
                "source_package_id": imported["package"]["id"],
                "package_truth_contract_key": "design_case.material_aggregate.v1",
            },
        ).json()["id"]
        with sessions() as db:
            item_id = db.scalars(select(BaselineSetItem)).one().id

        conflict = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-sets/{set_id}/items/{item_id}/truth",
            json={
                "edit_key": "material-text-2",
                "reason": "只改了文字没改计数",
                "truth_contract_key": "design_case.material_aggregate.v1",
                "expected_result": {"image_total_text": "16张", "image_total": 15},
            },
        )
        assert conflict.status_code == 422
        assert conflict.json()["detail"]["code"] == "design_case_aggregate_truth_conflict"

        # 反解不出来的写法当场拒，不会悄悄存成 0 张
        garbled = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-sets/{set_id}/items/{item_id}/truth",
            json={
                "edit_key": "material-text-3",
                "reason": "写法不对",
                "truth_contract_key": "design_case.material_aggregate.v1",
                "expected_result": {
                    "image_total_text": "16张",
                    "score_distribution_text": "八十二分三张",
                },
            },
        )
        assert garbled.status_code == 422
        assert "不是分数" in garbled.json()["detail"]["message"]


def test_material_dimension_contract_is_listed_for_the_editor() -> None:
    with _client() as (client, _):
        catalog = client.get("/api/baseline-truth-contracts/design_case")
        assert catalog.status_code == 200, catalog.text
        items = catalog.json()["items"]
        aggregate = next(
            item for item in items
            if item["contract_key"] == "design_case.material_aggregate.v1"
        )
        # 与「字段真值（案例）」是两个种类，前端流程不能混淆
        assert aggregate["contract_kind"] == "design_case_aggregate"
        assert [field["key"] for field in aggregate["fields"]] == [
            "image_total_text",
            "score_distribution_text",
            "feature_texts.trait",
        ]
        assert aggregate["workflow_input_required"] is False

===== FILE backend/tests/test_design_case_sample_import.py =====
"""素材（案例）维度样本解析：两种输入形态按列自动判别，计数写法兼容真实样本。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.design_case_sample_import import (
    AGGREGATED,
    TEXT_ONLY,
    DesignCaseSampleError,
    build_image_summary,
    parse_case_block,
    parse_case_rows,
    parse_count_text,
)

FULL = """素材标题：谜舍设计 | 长沙罗森尼娜
            分类：餐饮店铺-蛋糕甜品
            标签：烘焙店,面包店设计
            风格：现代
            采集源发布时间：2025/02/26
            设计师/设计公司：谜舍设计工作室
            案例正文前500个字：在长沙这座城市，快节奏成为常态。
            案例正文后500个字：手工砖被应用在店内的重要视觉区域。
            城市：长沙
            图片总张图：16张
            所有表现图的分数：82分有3张,84分有3张,88分有4张
            所有图片的类型：实景照片有15张,技术图有1张"""

TEXT_ONLY_BLOCK = """素材标题：只有文本的案例
            分类：办公空间
            案例正文前500个字：这是正文。"""


def test_aggregated_input_is_detected_and_decoded() -> None:
    s = parse_case_block(FULL, row_number=1)
    assert s.ok and s.input_kind == AGGREGATED
    assert s.case["case_name"] == "谜舍设计 | 长沙罗森尼娜"
    assert s.case["class_name"] == "餐饮店铺-蛋糕甜品"
    assert s.case["designer_company"] == "谜舍设计工作室"
    assert s.case["country"] == "长沙"
    # 正文前后段并入同一字段，不各占一列
    assert "在长沙这座城市" in s.case["case_text"] and "手工砖" in s.case["case_text"]
    summary = s.image_summary
    assert summary["image_total"] == 16
    assert summary["score_distribution"] == {"82": 3, "84": 3, "88": 4}
    assert summary["feature_counts"]["trait"] == {"实景照片": 15, "技术图": 1}
    assert summary["feature_texts"]["trait"] == "实景照片有15张,技术图有1张"


def test_text_only_input_is_detected() -> None:
    s = parse_case_block(TEXT_ONLY_BLOCK, row_number=2)
    assert s.ok and s.input_kind == TEXT_ONLY and s.image_summary is None


def test_count_text_tolerates_real_world_writings() -> None:
    """真实样本里省略「有N张」表示 1 张，同名分段出现要合并。"""

    assert parse_count_text("实景照片有15张,技术图有1张") == [
        {"value": "实景照片", "count": 15},
        {"value": "技术图", "count": 1},
    ]
    assert parse_count_text("实景照片有30张,实景照片,技术图有2张") == [
        {"value": "实景照片", "count": 31},
        {"value": "技术图", "count": 2},
    ]
    assert parse_count_text("82分有3张,88分有4张")[0]["value"] == "82"
    assert parse_count_text("") == []
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseSampleError):
        parse_count_text("有3张")


def test_totals_must_not_contradict_distribution() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseSampleError) as err:
        build_image_summary({
            "image_total_text": "5张",
            "score_distribution_text": "80分有4张,90分有4张",
        })
    assert "超过图片总数" in str(err.value)


def test_missing_body_warns_but_stays_usable() -> None:
    """正文为空但标题标签齐全的案例仍可用于文本评测，只提醒。"""

    s = parse_case_block("素材标题：无正文案例\n            分类：办公空间", row_number=3)
    assert s.ok and s.warnings and "正文" in s.warnings[0]


def test_missing_title_is_rejected() -> None:
    s = parse_case_block("分类：办公空间\n            案例正文前500个字：正文", row_number=4)
    assert not s.ok and "缺少素材标题" in s.issues[0]


def test_plan_counts_each_input_kind() -> None:
    plan = parse_case_rows([FULL, TEXT_ONLY_BLOCK, "分类：无标题"])
    assert plan["total_rows"] == 3
    assert plan["usable_rows"] == 2 and plan["rejected_rows"] == 1
    assert plan["input_kinds"] == {AGGREGATED: 1, TEXT_ONLY: 2}


def test_score_distribution_values_must_be_numbers() -> None:
    """省略计数的写法只对类型分布成立，套到分数上会存出假分档。"""

    import pytest

    from app.design_case_sample_import import DesignCaseSampleError, build_image_summary

    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseSampleError) as err:
        build_image_summary(
            {"image_total_text": "16张", "score_distribution_text": "八十二分三张"}
        )
    assert err.value.code == "design_case_sample_score_invalid"

    # 类型分布仍然接受省略计数的写法（真实样本里就有）
    summary = build_image_summary(
        {"image_total_text": "16张", "type_distribution_text": "实景照片,技术图有1张"}
    )
    assert summary["feature_counts"]["trait"] == {"实景照片": 1, "技术图": 1}

===== FILE backend/tests/test_design_case_sample_service.py =====
"""案例样本落库：进素材库、可去重、保留两种输入形态。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.design_case_sample_import import parse_case_block
from app.design_case_sample_service import (
    CASE_MIME_TYPE,
    CASE_STORAGE_BACKEND,
    DesignCaseSampleServiceError,
    case_content_hash,
    import_case_samples,
)
from app.models import Asset, DesignCaseSample

AGG = """素材标题：谜舍设计 | 长沙罗森尼娜
            分类：餐饮店铺-蛋糕甜品
            案例正文前500个字：正文内容。
            图片总张图：16张
            所有表现图的分数：82分有3张,88分有4张
            所有图片的类型：实景照片有15张,技术图有1张"""

TXT = """素材标题：只有文本的案例
            分类：办公空间
            案例正文前500个字：另一段正文。"""


def _samples(*blocks: str):
    return [parse_case_block(b, row_number=i + 1) for i, b in enumerate(blocks)]


def _session(tmp_path, name="case.db"):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / name}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    return engine, Session(engine)


def test_case_samples_land_as_assets_with_payload(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine, db = _session(tmp_path)
    try:
        result = import_case_samples(
            db, samples=_samples(AGG, TXT),
            category_key="design_case", package_name="案例样本-测试", actor="sol",
        )
        db.commit()
        assert result["summary"] == {
            "created_count": 2, "duplicate_count": 0,
            "aggregated_count": 1, "warned_count": 0,
        }
        assets = db.scalars(select(Asset)).all()
        assert len(assets) == 2
        for asset in assets:
            # 案例素材没有图片文件：不占存储、没有宽高
            assert asset.mime_type == CASE_MIME_TYPE
            assert asset.storage_backend == CASE_STORAGE_BACKEND
            assert asset.width is None and asset.height is None
            assert asset.category_key == "design_case"
        records = db.scalars(select(DesignCaseSample)).all()
        agg = next(r for r in records if r.input_kind == "aggregated")
        case = json.loads(agg.case_json)
        summary = json.loads(agg.image_summary_json)
        assert case["case_name"] == "谜舍设计 | 长沙罗森尼娜"
        assert summary["image_total"] == 16
        assert summary["score_distribution"] == {"82": 3, "88": 4}
        assert summary["feature_counts"]["trait"] == {"实景照片": 15, "技术图": 1}
        # 每条案例都记得来自表格哪一行，便于运营对账
        assert {r.source_row_number for r in records} == {1, 2}
        assert all(r.source_package_id == result["package"]["id"] for r in records)
    finally:
        db.close(); engine.dispose()


def test_same_case_is_not_imported_twice(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine, db = _session(tmp_path, "dup.db")
    try:
        import_case_samples(db, samples=_samples(AGG), category_key="design_case",
                            package_name=None, actor="sol")
        db.commit()
        again = import_case_samples(db, samples=_samples(AGG), category_key="design_case",
                                    package_name=None, actor="sol")
        db.commit()
        assert again["summary"]["created_count"] == 0
        assert again["duplicates"][0]["reason"] == "素材库已存在相同案例"
        assert len(db.scalars(select(Asset)).all()) == 1
    finally:
        db.close(); engine.dispose()


def test_duplicates_inside_one_upload_are_collapsed(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine, db = _session(tmp_path, "self.db")
    try:
        result = import_case_samples(db, samples=_samples(AGG, AGG),
                                     category_key="design_case", package_name=None, actor="sol")
        db.commit()
        assert result["summary"]["created_count"] == 1
        assert result["duplicates"][0]["reason"] == "本次导入内重复"
    finally:
        db.close(); engine.dispose()


def test_content_hash_ignores_row_order_but_tracks_content() -> None:
    a = parse_case_block(AGG, row_number=1)
    b = parse_case_block(AGG, row_number=99)
    assert case_content_hash(a.case, a.image_summary) == case_content_hash(b.case, b.image_summary)
    c = parse_case_block(AGG.replace("16张", "17张"), row_number=1)
    assert case_content_hash(a.case, a.image_summary) != case_content_hash(c.case, c.image_summary)


def test_empty_import_is_rejected(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine, db = _session(tmp_path, "empty.db")
    try:
        with pytest.raises(DesignCaseSampleServiceError) as err:
            import_case_samples(db, samples=[], category_key="design_case",
                                package_name=None, actor="sol")
        assert err.value.code == "design_case_sample_empty"
    finally:
        db.close(); engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_design_case_sample_xlsx.py =====
"""从运营的表格里定位案例列：无表头、多余列、空格都要能扛。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.design_case_sample_import import DesignCaseSampleError, parse_case_rows
from app.design_case_sample_xlsx import read_case_texts
from test_p0e_safe_import import _write_xlsx

CASE_A = (
    "素材标题：谜舍设计 | 长沙罗森尼娜\n"
    "分类：餐饮店铺-蛋糕甜品\n"
    "案例正文前500个字：正文。\n"
    "图片总张图：16张\n"
    "所有表现图的分数：82分有3张,88分有4张\n"
    "所有图片的类型：实景照片有15张,技术图有1张"
)
CASE_B = "素材标题：只有文本的案例\n分类：办公空间\n案例正文前500个字：另一段正文。"


def test_first_row_may_already_be_data(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "no-header.xlsx"
    _write_xlsx(path, [[CASE_A], [CASE_B]])
    extracted = read_case_texts(path)
    assert extracted["header_is_data"] is True
    assert [row for row, _ in extracted["texts"]] == [1, 2]


def test_header_row_is_skipped_when_present(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "header.xlsx"
    _write_xlsx(path, [["案例样本"], [CASE_A], [CASE_B]])
    extracted = read_case_texts(path)
    assert extracted["header_is_data"] is False
    # 行号沿用表格行号，运营在 Excel 里能直接跳到对应行
    assert [row for row, _ in extracted["texts"]] == [2, 3]


def test_case_column_is_found_among_other_columns(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "wide.xlsx"
    _write_xlsx(path, [["序号", "备注", "案例"], [1, "无关", CASE_A], [2, "无关", CASE_B]])
    extracted = read_case_texts(path)
    assert extracted["column_number"] == 3
    plan = parse_case_rows(extracted["texts"])
    assert plan["usable_rows"] == 2
    assert plan["input_kinds"] == {"aggregated": 1, "text_only": 1}


def test_blank_cells_are_reported_not_parsed(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "blank.xlsx"
    _write_xlsx(path, [[CASE_A], [""], [CASE_B]])
    extracted = read_case_texts(path)
    assert extracted["empty_rows"] == [2]
    assert len(extracted["texts"]) == 2


def test_table_without_case_column_is_rejected(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "unrelated.xlsx"
    _write_xlsx(path, [["名称", "数量"], ["A", 1]])
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseSampleError) as err:
        read_case_texts(path)
    assert err.value.code == "design_case_sample_column_not_found"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_dictionary_filter.py =====
"""字典表撞库过滤：命中按规范词回写，未命中收集；内容校验 fail-closed。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.dictionary_filter import (
    DictionaryFilterError,
    apply_dictionary_filters,
    validate_dictionary_filter_content,
)
from app.evaluation_component_catalog import EvaluationComponentError, validate_module_content


def _manifest(content: dict) -> dict:
    return {
        "nodes": [
            {"node_instance_id": "dict_1", "kind": "rule_module",
             "config": {"module_type_key": "dictionary_filter", "component_snapshot": {"content": content}}},
            {"node_instance_id": "prompt_a", "kind": "prompt", "config": {}},
        ]
    }


def _content(**overrides) -> dict:
    base = {
        "format_version": "dictionary-filter-v1",
        "enabled": True,
        "field_paths": ["tags", "production.style"],
        "match_mode": "alias",
        "case_insensitive": True,
        "on_miss": "collect",
        "entries": [
            {"canonical": "北欧简约", "aliases": ["北欧风", "北欧"]},
            {"canonical": "木质家具", "aliases": []},
        ],
    }
    base.update(overrides)
    return base


def test_hits_are_canonicalized_and_misses_collected() -> None:
    normalized = {"tags": ["北欧风", "木质家具", "海景", "北欧"], "production": {"style": "北欧, 工业风"}, "score": 78}
    result, misses = apply_dictionary_filters(_manifest(_content()), normalized)
    assert result["tags"] == ["北欧简约", "木质家具"]
    assert result["production"]["style"] == "北欧简约"
    assert result["score"] == 78
    assert misses == [
        {"node": "dict_1", "field_path": "tags", "term": "海景"},
        {"node": "dict_1", "field_path": "production.style", "term": "工业风"},
    ]
    audit = result["dictionary_filter"]
    assert audit[0]["unmatched"] == ["海景"] and audit[1]["matched"] == ["北欧简约"]


def test_exact_mode_ignores_aliases_and_drop_mode_skips_collection() -> None:
    content = _content(match_mode="exact", on_miss="drop")
    result, misses = apply_dictionary_filters(_manifest(content), {"tags": ["北欧风", "北欧简约"]})
    assert result["tags"] == ["北欧简约"] and misses == []


def test_disabled_or_absent_module_is_noop() -> None:
    normalized = {"tags": ["x"]}
    result, misses = apply_dictionary_filters(_manifest(_content(enabled=False)), normalized)
    assert result == normalized and misses == []
    result, misses = apply_dictionary_filters({"nodes": []}, normalized)
    assert result == normalized and misses == []


def test_content_validation_rejects_duplicates_and_bad_modes() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(DictionaryFilterError) as exc:
        validate_dictionary_filter_content(_content(entries=[{"canonical": "a", "aliases": ["A"]}]))
    assert exc.value.code == "entry_duplicate"
    with pytest.raises(DictionaryFilterError):
        validate_dictionary_filter_content(_content(match_mode="fuzzy"))
    with pytest.raises(DictionaryFilterError):
        validate_dictionary_filter_content(_content(field_paths=[]))
    normalized = validate_module_content("dictionary_filter", _content())
    assert normalized["entries"][0]["canonical"] == "北欧简约"
    with pytest.raises(EvaluationComponentError):
        validate_module_content("dictionary_filter", _content(on_miss="ignore"))


# --- 未命中词入库（workflow_result_finalizer._record_dictionary_misses） ---

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker


@pytest.fixture
def miss_sessions(tmp_path):
    from app.models import DictionaryMissCandidate

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'misses.db'}",
                           connect_args={"timeout": 10})
    DictionaryMissCandidate.__table__.create(engine)
    try:
        # 与生产 SessionLocal 一致：autoflush 关闭。
        yield sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False, autoflush=False)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _record(db, *blocks, run_id=1):
    from app.workflow_result_finalizer import _record_dictionary_misses

    run = SimpleNamespace(id=run_id, category_key="inspiration")
    _record_dictionary_misses(db, run, {"dictionary_filter": list(blocks)},
                              now=datetime.now(timezone.utc))


def _rows(sessions):
    from app.models import DictionaryMissCandidate

    with sessions() as db:
        return sorted((r.field_path, r.term, r.hit_count)
                      for r in db.scalars(select(DictionaryMissCandidate)))


def test_duplicate_unmatched_terms_in_one_result_do_not_break_finalization(miss_sessions):
    """同一结果里重复的未命中词（含只差大小写的）在 autoflush 关闭时会撞唯一约束。"""

    with miss_sessions() as db:
        _record(db,
                {"field_path": "tags", "unmatched": ["Loft", "loft", " Loft ", "工业风"]},
                {"field_path": "tags", "unmatched": ["工业风"]})
        db.commit()
    assert _rows(miss_sessions) == [("tags", "Loft", 1), ("tags", "工业风", 1)]


def test_existing_candidate_is_counted_atomically(miss_sessions):
    """两个收尾事务同时见到同一个词，计数不能丢。"""

    import threading
    import time

    with miss_sessions() as db:
        _record(db, {"field_path": "tags", "unmatched": ["工业风"]})
        db.commit()
    first_recorded = threading.Event()
    errors = []

    def first():
        try:
            with miss_sessions() as db:
                _record(db, {"field_path": "tags", "unmatched": ["工业风"]}, run_id=2)
                first_recorded.set()
                time.sleep(0.3)
                db.commit()
        except BaseException as exc:  # pragma: no cover - surfaced below
            errors.append(exc)
            first_recorded.set()

    def second():
        try:
            first_recorded.wait(5)
            with miss_sessions() as db:
                _record(db, {"field_path": "tags", "unmatched": ["工业风"]}, run_id=3)
                db.commit()
        except BaseException as exc:  # pragma: no cover
            errors.append(exc)

    threads = [threading.Thread(target=first), threading.Thread(target=second)]
    for thread in threads:
        thread.start()
    for thread in threads:
        thread.join(15)
    assert errors == []
    assert _rows(miss_sessions) == [("tags", "工业风", 3)]


def test_concurrent_insert_of_same_term_falls_back_to_increment(miss_sessions, monkeypatch):
    """另一个事务先插入了同一个词：唯一约束冲突要转为累加，不让收尾崩溃。"""

    import app.workflow_result_finalizer as finalizer
    from app.models import DictionaryMissCandidate

    with miss_sessions() as db:
        db.add(DictionaryMissCandidate(category_key="inspiration", field_path="tags",
                                       term="工业风", hit_count=1, status="pending"))
        db.commit()
    if hasattr(finalizer, "_bump_dictionary_miss"):
        real = finalizer._bump_dictionary_miss
        calls = {"n": 0}

        def racing_bump(*args, **kwargs):
            calls["n"] += 1
            if calls["n"] == 1:
                return False  # 本事务查时还没有，插入前别人已经提交
            return real(*args, **kwargs)

        monkeypatch.setattr(finalizer, "_bump_dictionary_miss", racing_bump)
    with miss_sessions() as db:
        _record(db, {"field_path": "tags", "unmatched": ["工业风", "新词"]}, run_id=2)
        db.commit()
    assert _rows(miss_sessions) == [("tags", "工业风", 2), ("tags", "新词", 1)]
    if hasattr(finalizer, "_bump_dictionary_miss"):
        assert calls["n"] >= 2


def test_overlong_unmatched_term_is_skipped_not_fatal(miss_sessions):
    with miss_sessions() as db:
        _record(db, {"field_path": "tags", "unmatched": ["长" * 201, "短词"]})
        db.commit()
    assert _rows(miss_sessions) == [("tags", "短词", 1)]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_dimension_bound_regression.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from copy import deepcopy
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app.dimension_schema_registry import canonical_hash
from app.regression import (
    compare_paired_results,
    result_fields,
    reviewed_truth_snapshot,
)


def _definition(*, label_suffix: str = "") -> dict:
    keys = (
        "presentation_integrity",
        "visual_hierarchy",
        "inspiration_reference",
    )
    return {
        "format_version": "dimension-schema-definition-v1",
        "dimensions": [
            {
                "key": key,
                "label": f"{key}{label_suffix}",
                "aggregation_role": "score",
            }
            for key in keys
        ],
        "output_contract": {
            "dimension_output_keys": list(keys),
            "unknown_key_policy": "reject",
        },
    }


def _result(
    result_id: int,
    *,
    definition: dict,
    schema_id: int = 11,
    schema_version: str = "candidate-v1",
    strategy_version: str = "strategy-bundle-v2",
) -> SimpleNamespace:
    dimensions = {
        dimension["key"]: {"grade": 3}
        for dimension in definition["dimensions"]
    }
    dimensions["legacy_extra_dimension"] = {"grade": 5}
    snapshot = {"schema_version": strategy_version}
    if strategy_version == "strategy-bundle-v2":
        snapshot.update(
            {
                "resolved_dimension_schema_id": schema_id,
                "resolved_dimension_schema_key": "dimension.candidate",
                "resolved_dimension_schema_version": schema_version,
                "resolved_dimension_schema_hash": canonical_hash(definition),
                "resolved_dimensions_snapshot": deepcopy(definition),
            }
        )
    return SimpleNamespace(
        id=result_id,
        asset_id=1,
        strategy_bundle_id=result_id,
        strategy_snapshot_json=json.dumps(snapshot, ensure_ascii=False),
        precheck_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "classification": {
                    "scope_status": "in_scope",
                    "primary_category": "测试素材",
                },
                "image_quality": {"quality_severity": "normal"},
                "media_form": {},
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
        aesthetic_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "dimensions": dimensions,
                "decision_rules": {
                    "hard_gate_triggered": False,
                    "hard_gate_target": "none",
                    "level_cap": "none",
                },
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
        scoring_json=json.dumps(
            {"formal": True, "caps": []},
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
        level="L3",
        score=65.0,
        needs_review=False,
        reviews=[],
        review_stage="completed",
        review_revision=0,
    )


def _approve(result: SimpleNamespace) -> None:
    result.reviews = [
        SimpleNamespace(
            id=21,
            reviewer_name="reviewer",
            decision="approved",
            corrected_level=None,
            corrected_score=None,
            corrections_json="[]",
            note="confirmed",
            created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )
    ]


def test_result_contract_uses_only_bound_dimension_keys() -> None:
    definition = _definition()
    fields = result_fields(_result(1, definition=definition))

    assert tuple(fields["dimensions"]) == (
        "presentation_integrity",
        "visual_hierarchy",
        "inspiration_reference",
    )
    assert "legacy_extra_dimension" not in fields["dimensions"]
    assert fields["dimension_schema"] == {
        "binding_version": "dimension-truth-binding-v1",
        "schema_id": 11,
        "schema_key": "dimension.candidate",
        "version": "candidate-v1",
        "canonical_hash": canonical_hash(definition),
        "definition": definition,
    }


def test_result_contract_maps_legacy_alias_to_bound_dimension_key() -> None:
    definition = _definition()
    result = _result(1, definition=definition)
    aesthetic = json.loads(result.aesthetic_json)
    aesthetic["dimensions"]["contemporary_relevance"] = (
        aesthetic["dimensions"].pop("inspiration_reference")
    )
    result.aesthetic_json = json.dumps(aesthetic, ensure_ascii=False)

    fields = result_fields(result)

    assert fields["dimensions"]["inspiration_reference"] == 3
    assert "contemporary_relevance" not in fields["dimensions"]


def test_reviewed_truth_v2_freezes_full_dimension_identity() -> None:
    definition = _definition()
    result = _result(1, definition=definition)
    _approve(result)

    snapshot, _review = reviewed_truth_snapshot(result, "stable_control")

    assert snapshot["schema_version"] == "paired-truth-v2"
    assert snapshot["truth"]["dimension_schema"]["definition"] == definition
    assert (
        snapshot["truth"]["dimension_schema"]["canonical_hash"]
        == canonical_hash(definition)
    )


def test_paired_regression_rejects_different_dimension_semantics() -> None:
    baseline_definition = _definition()
    candidate_definition = _definition(label_suffix="-changed")
    baseline = _result(1, definition=baseline_definition)
    candidate = _result(
        2,
        definition=candidate_definition,
        schema_id=12,
        schema_version="candidate-v2",
    )
    _approve(baseline)
    truth_snapshot, _review = reviewed_truth_snapshot(
        baseline,
        "stable_control",
    )

    comparison = compare_paired_results(
        truth_snapshot=truth_snapshot,
        role="stable_control",
        baseline=baseline,
        candidate=candidate,
    )

    assert comparison["passed"] is False
    assert comparison["dimension_schema_mismatches"] == [
        "candidate.dimension_schema.schema_id",
        "candidate.dimension_schema.version",
        "candidate.dimension_schema.canonical_hash",
    ]
    assert comparison["failure_reasons"][0]["code"] == (
        "dimension_schema_mismatch"
    )


def test_paired_regression_rejects_tampered_truth_definition() -> None:
    definition = _definition()
    baseline = _result(1, definition=definition)
    _approve(baseline)
    truth_snapshot, _review = reviewed_truth_snapshot(
        baseline,
        "stable_control",
    )
    truth_snapshot["truth"]["dimension_schema"]["definition"][
        "dimensions"
    ][0]["label"] = "tampered"

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="无法复算",
    ):
        compare_paired_results(
            truth_snapshot=truth_snapshot,
            role="stable_control",
            baseline=baseline,
            candidate=_result(2, definition=definition),
        )


def test_v1_review_truth_remains_readable_without_schema_identity() -> None:
    result = _result(
        1,
        definition=_definition(),
        strategy_version="strategy-bundle-v1",
    )
    _approve(result)

    snapshot, _review = reviewed_truth_snapshot(result, "stable_control")

    assert snapshot["schema_version"] == "paired-truth-v1"
    assert "dimension_schema" not in snapshot["truth"]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_dimension_calibration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select

    DimensionCalibrationContractError,
    DimensionCalibrationStateError,
    claim_dimension_calibration_item,
    complete_dimension_calibration_item,
    create_dimension_calibration_run,
    fail_dimension_calibration_item,

    CORE_SCHEMA_KEY,
    CORE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    PRODUCT_SCHEMA_KEY,

@pytest.fixture
def database(tmp_path) -> tuple[Session, object]:
    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'dimension-calibration.db'}",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False, "timeout": 10},
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)
    session = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False, autoflush=False)
    try:
        yield session, engine
    finally:

        engine.dispose()


def _seed_inputs(
    db: Session,
) -> tuple[
    ModelConfig,
    PromptVersion,
    PromptVersion,
    PromptVersion,
    SamplingPolicy,
]:
    model = ModelConfig(
        name="Calibration Model",
        provider="doubao",
        base_url="https://example.test/v1",
        api_path="/chat/completions",
        model_id="calibration-model",
        encrypted_api_key="encrypted-placeholder",
        temperature=0.1,
        max_tokens=4096,
        timeout_seconds=120,
        max_retries=1,
        max_concurrency=2,
        structured_output=True,
        high_risk_review_enabled=True,
    )
    prompt_a = PromptVersion(
        stage="A",
        name="Calibration A",
        version="calibration-A-v1",
        system_prompt="System A",
        user_prompt="User A {{image_metadata}}",
        rubric_version="calibration-rubric-v1",
        status="published",
    )
    space_b = PromptVersion(
        stage="B",
        name="Space B",
        version="space-B-v1",
        system_prompt="System Space B",
        user_prompt="Space {{precheck_json}} {{rubric_version}}",
        rubric_version="space-rubric-v1",
        status="published",
    )
    product_b = PromptVersion(
        stage="B",
        name="Product B candidate",
        version="product-B-v0.1-candidate.1",
        system_prompt="System Product B",
        user_prompt="Product {{precheck_json}} {{rubric_version}}",
        rubric_version="product-rubric-v0.1",
        status="draft",
    )
    sampling = SamplingPolicy(
        id=1,
        revision=1,
        sample_rate=10,
        low_confidence_threshold=0.7,
        medium_confidence_threshold=0.9,
        cold_start_required_count=5,
        high_level_required_from=4,
    )
    db.add_all([model, prompt_a, space_b, product_b, sampling])
    db.commit()
    return model, prompt_a, space_b, product_b, sampling


def _registry(
    db: Session,
) -> tuple[
    DimensionRoutePolicy,
    DimensionSchema,
    DimensionSchema,
    DimensionSchema,
]:
    policy = db.scalar(
        select(DimensionRoutePolicy).where(
            DimensionRoutePolicy.policy_key == ROUTE_POLICY_KEY,
            DimensionRoutePolicy.version == ROUTE_POLICY_VERSION,
        )
    )
    space = db.scalar(
        select(DimensionSchema).where(
            DimensionSchema.schema_key == SPACE_SCHEMA_KEY,
            DimensionSchema.version == ACTIVE_V13_VERSION,
        )
    )
    core = db.scalar(
        select(DimensionSchema).where(
            DimensionSchema.schema_key == CORE_SCHEMA_KEY,
            DimensionSchema.version == CORE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        )
    )
    product = db.scalar(
        select(DimensionSchema).where(
            DimensionSchema.schema_key == PRODUCT_SCHEMA_KEY,
            DimensionSchema.version == PRODUCT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        )
    )
    assert policy and space and core and product
    return policy, space, core, product


def _routed_bundle(
    db: Session,
    *,
    include_product_prompt: bool = True,
) -> tuple[StrategyBundle, PromptVersion, SamplingPolicy]:
    model, prompt_a, space_b, product_b, sampling = _seed_inputs(db)
    policy, space, core, product = _registry(db)
    profile_set = build_evaluation_profile_set(
        profiles=[
            build_frozen_evaluation_profile(
                profile_key="space",
                schema=space,
                prompt_b=space_b,
            ),
            build_frozen_evaluation_profile(
                profile_key="common",
                schema=core,
                prompt_b=None,
            ),
            build_frozen_evaluation_profile(
                profile_key="product",
                schema=product,
                prompt_b=(
                    product_b if include_product_prompt else None
                ),
            ),
        ],
        execution_context="calibration",
        default_profile_key="common",
    )
    bundle = get_or_create_routed_bundle(
        db=db,
        model_config=model,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        route_policy=policy,
        evaluation_profile_set=profile_set,
        engine_version=ENGINE_VERSION,
        risk_review_version=None,
        sampling_policy=sampling,
    )
    db.commit()
    return bundle, prompt_a, sampling


def _asset(db: Session, number: int, *, status: str = "uploaded") -> Asset:
    asset = Asset(
        original_name=f"asset-{number}.jpg",
        stored_name=f"asset-{number}.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=100 + number,
        width=1000,
        height=800,
        sha256=f"{number:064x}",
        status=status,
    )
    db.add(asset)
    db.commit()
    return asset


def _product_precheck() -> dict:
    return {
        "classification": {
            "scope_status": "boundary",
            "primary_category": "软装家具",
            "primary_confidence": 0.92,
        },
        "scene_scope": {"type": "object_only"},
        "media_form": {
            "white_background_product": {
                "status": "yes",
                "confidence": 0.95,
            }
        },
        "image_quality": {
            "quality_severity": "normal",
            "confidence": 0.95,
            "evidence": [],
        },
        "needs_review": False,
        "review_reasons": [],
    }


def _precheck_for(
    *,
    category: str,
    quality: str = "normal",
) -> dict:
    payload = _product_precheck()
    payload["classification"]["primary_category"] = category
    payload["scene_scope"]["type"] = (
        "uncertain" if category in {"平面设计", "意向图"} else "object_only"
    )
    payload["media_form"]["white_background_product"]["status"] = (
        "no" if category in {"平面设计", "意向图"} else "yes"
    )
    payload["image_quality"]["quality_severity"] = quality
    return payload


def _product_aesthetic() -> dict:
    keys = (
        "presentation_integrity",
        "visual_hierarchy",
        "inspiration_reference",
        "product_form_proportion",
        "material_craft_detail",
        "functional_clarity",
        "scene_styling_fit",
    )
    return {
        "dimensions": {
            key: {
                "grade": 3,
                "evidence": [f"{key} 可见证据"],
                "reason": f"{key} 基础成立",
            }
            for key in keys
        },
        "assessment_confidence": 0.88,
        "needs_review": False,
        "review_reasons": [],
    }


def _run(
    db: Session,
    *,
    bundle: StrategyBundle,
    assets: list[Asset],
    run_key: str,
) -> DimensionCalibrationRun:
    run = create_dimension_calibration_run(
        db,
        run_key=run_key,
        strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
        asset_ids=[asset.id for asset in assets],
        created_by="owner",
    )
    db.commit()
    return run


def _resolution(
    *,
    bundle: StrategyBundle,
    prompt_a: PromptVersion,
    sampling: SamplingPolicy,
    precheck: dict,
    now: datetime,
) -> str:
    return build_routed_evaluation_strategy_snapshot(
        bundle=bundle,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        sampling_policy=sampling,
        precheck=precheck,
        resolution_timestamp=now,
    )


def test_create_run_is_idempotent_and_conflict_safe(database) -> None:
    db, _engine = database
    bundle, _prompt_a, _sampling = _routed_bundle(db)
    first = _asset(db, 1)
    second = _asset(db, 2)
    run = _run(
        db,
        bundle=bundle,
        assets=[first],
        run_key="calibration:stable",
    )
    replay = create_dimension_calibration_run(
        db,
        run_key="calibration:stable",
        strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
        asset_ids=[first.id],
        created_by="another-user",
    )
    assert replay.id == run.id
    assert len(run.items) == 1
    with pytest.raises(
        DimensionCalibrationContractError,
        match="已绑定不同定义",
    ):
        create_dimension_calibration_run(
            db,
            run_key="calibration:stable",
            strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
            asset_ids=[second.id],
            created_by="owner",
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("asset_ids", [[], list(range(1, 102))])

    db, _engine = database

            db,

            strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,

        )

    bundle, _prompt_a, _sampling = _routed_bundle(db)

    ):
        create_dimension_calibration_run(
            db,
            run_key="calibration:duplicate",
            strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
            asset_ids=[asset.id, asset.id],
            created_by="owner",
        )
    unavailable = _asset(db, 2, status="queued")

        DimensionCalibrationContractError,
        match="状态不可用",
    ):
        create_dimension_calibration_run(
            db,
            run_key="calibration:unavailable",
            strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
            asset_ids=[unavailable.id],
            created_by="owner",
        )

def test_create_run_rejects_production_context(database) -> None:
    db, _engine = database
    bundle, _prompt_a, _sampling = _routed_bundle(db)
    asset = _asset(db, 1)
    original = bundle.evaluation_profile_set_snapshot
    payload = json.loads(original)
    payload["execution_context"] = "production"
    payload["canonical_hash"] = canonical_hash(
        {
            key: value
            for key, value in payload.items()
            if key != "canonical_hash"
        }
    )
    bundle.evaluation_profile_set_snapshot = canonical_json(payload)
    try:
        with pytest.raises(DimensionCalibrationContractError):
            create_dimension_calibration_run(
                db,
                run_key="calibration:production",
                strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
                asset_ids=[asset.id],
                created_by="owner",
            )
    finally:
        bundle.evaluation_profile_set_snapshot = original


def test_claim_is_conditional_and_single_owner(database) -> None:
    db, engine = database
    bundle, _prompt_a, _sampling = _routed_bundle(db)
    asset = _asset(db, 1)
    run = _run(
        db,
        bundle=bundle,
        assets=[asset],
        run_key="calibration:claim",
    )
    first = claim_dimension_calibration_item(
        db,
        run_id=run.id,
        worker_id="worker-1",
    )
    assert first is not None
    db.commit()
    with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False, autoflush=False) as second_db:
        assert claim_dimension_calibration_item(
            second_db,
            run_id=run.id,
            worker_id="worker-2",
        ) is None
    assert first.status == "processing"
    assert run.status == "running"
    assert run.processing == 1


def test_completed_result_is_isolated_and_replay_verified(database) -> None:
    db, _engine = database
    bundle, prompt_a, sampling = _routed_bundle(db)
    asset = _asset(db, 1)
    run = _run(
        db,
        bundle=bundle,
        assets=[asset],
        run_key="calibration:completed",
    )
    item = claim_dimension_calibration_item(
        db,
        run_id=run.id,
        worker_id="worker-1",
    )
    assert item is not None
    db.commit()
    now = datetime(2026, 7, 31, 6, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    precheck = _product_precheck()
    result = complete_dimension_calibration_item(
        db,
        item_id=item.id,
        worker_id="worker-1",
        terminal_status="completed",
        precheck=precheck,
        resolution_snapshot=_resolution(
            bundle=bundle,
            prompt_a=prompt_a,
            sampling=sampling,
            precheck=precheck,
            now=now,
        ),
        aesthetic=_product_aesthetic(),
        raw_response_a='{"stage":"A"}',
        raw_response_b='{"stage":"B"}',
        now=now,
    )
    db.commit()
    assert result.status == "completed"
    assert result.level in {"L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"}
    assert result.score is not None
    assert run.status == "completed"
    assert run.completed == 1
    assert run.finished_at is not None
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count(EvaluationJob.id))) == 0
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count(EvaluationResult.id))) == 0
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count(HumanReview.id))) == 0
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ReviewPanel.id))) == 0


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("category", "quality", "terminal_status"),
    [
        ("平面设计", "normal", "core_fallback"),
        ("软装家具", "unusable", "unassessable"),
    ],
)
def test_non_resolved_business_outcomes_do_not_store_scores(
    database,
    category: str,
    quality: str,
    terminal_status: str,
) -> None:

    bundle, prompt_a, sampling = _routed_bundle(db)

    )

        db,
        run_id=run.id,
        worker_id="worker-1",
    )
    assert item is not None
    now = datetime(2026, 7, 31, 6, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    precheck = _precheck_for(category=category, quality=quality)
    result = complete_dimension_calibration_item(
        db,
        item_id=item.id,

    assert result.scoring_json is None
    assert result.score is None
    assert result.level is None
    assert result.confidence is None
